Learning Path 01

Infrastructuur, identiteit en inschrijving

5 modules · Nederlands met Engelstalige cursusverdieping

Learning Path 01Module 01

Endpointmanagement en Microsoft Intune

Je leert hoe cloudbeheer, apparaatidentiteit en toegangsbeleid samen een moderne werkplek ondersteunen.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Een beheerkeuze bepaalt welke infrastructuur nodig is, wie instellingen beheert en hoe je bedrijfsgegevens beschermt op kantoor en daarbuiten.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: de beheerketen begrijpen.
  • Uitleggen: intune, Configuration Manager en co-management.
  • Uitleggen: cloud-only, hybride en BYOD.
  • Uitleggen: mDM-acties verantwoord gebruiken.

01. De beheerketen begrijpen

Intune levert apparaatbeheer (MDM) en applicatiebeheer (MAM) vanuit de cloud. Apparaten communiceren via internet met de dienst en ontvangen toegewezen instellingen en apps. Microsoft Entra ID levert gebruikers, groepen en apparaatidentiteiten. Identiteit, beheer en beveiliging werken samen, maar hebben elk een eigen taak.

02. Intune, Configuration Manager en co-management

Configuration Manager ondersteunt bestaande lokale beheerprocessen met een client en infrastructuur. Intune richt zich op cloudbeheer van meerdere platformen. Co-management combineert beide voor ondersteunde Windows-apparaten: workloads verschuiven gecontroleerd naar Intune. Plan per workload welk systeem verantwoordelijk is en voorkom tegenstrijdig beleid.

03. Cloud-only, hybride en BYOD

Cloud-only apparaten kunnen rechtstreeks aan Entra ID gekoppeld worden. Hybride scenario’s behouden afhankelijkheden van lokaal Active Directory. Bij BYOD is het apparaat persoonlijk eigendom. Kies daar bewust tussen volledige inschrijving en bescherming van alleen bedrijfsgegevens in ondersteunde apps. Leg privacy en beheerbevoegdheden vooraf uit.

04. MDM-acties verantwoord gebruiken

Met MDM inventariseer je apparaten, distribueer je configuratie en voer je remote acties uit. Controleer eigendom, platform en gevolgen voordat je retire of wipe gebruikt. MAM kan zakelijke data selectief beschermen of verwijderen zonder het hele persoonlijke apparaat te wissen. Een goede beheerstrategie beschrijft ook uitdienstname.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • MDM beheert apparaten; MAM beschermt bedrijfsgegevens in apps.
  • Entra ID levert identiteit; Intune levert beheer.
  • Verschuif workloads met een pilot en duidelijk eigenaarschap.

Samenvatting

MDM beheert apparaten; MAM beschermt bedrijfsgegevens in apps. Entra ID levert identiteit; Intune levert beheer. Verschuif workloads met een pilot en duidelijk eigenaarschap. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Cursusverdieping per onderwerp (Engels) · 7 bronslides

Original course content. Text order follows the source presentation; visual tables and diagrams are represented as text.

Slide 5

What is Microsoft Intune and how does it work?

  • Microsoft Intune
  • Cloud endpoint management · Microsoft 365 cloud + Microsoft Entra ID
  • Managed from the cloud
  • Devices, apps, and security policies from one place
  • Configuration and policy stored in the cloud
  • Endpoints communicate over the internet
  • MDM : the device
  • Applies settings and policies to the whole device
  • Controls OS updates, security, inventory
  • Best for corporate-owned, enrolled devices
  • MAM : data in apps
  • Protects corporate data inside apps, not the device
  • Works even when devices aren't enrolled
  • Ideal for BYOD; often paired with MDM
  • Windows
  • macOS
  • iOS / iPadOS
  • Android
  • Policies target user or device groups and deploy over HTTPS. Intune tracks compliance and inventory and supports remote actions such as wipe and lock : no VPN or on-premises servers.
Slide 6

How Microsoft Intune works : architecture and roles

  • How it works
  • Runs in the Microsoft 365 cloud, integrated with Microsoft Entra ID for identity and device registration
  • Devices enroll over secure HTTPS and download a management profile
  • Policies are assigned to user or device groups and delivered to endpoints
  • Intune tracks compliance, reports inventory, and supports remote wipe or lock
  • Who manages what
  • Role
  • Responsibility
  • Global administrator
  • Full access to all Intune and Entra ID settings
  • Intune administrator
  • Manages policies, apps, and devices
  • Compliance administrator
  • Builds and monitors compliance policies
  • RBAC
  • Restricts each admin to only relevant sections
  • Why it matters
  • Centralizing management in the cloud cuts complexity and cost for remote, multi-device workforces : enforcing security and deploying apps across Windows, macOS, iOS/iPadOS, and Android without VPN or on-premises infrastructure.
Slide 7

Compare Configuration Manager, Intune and co-management

  • Configuration Manager
  • Intune
  • Co-management
  • Device management
  • On-premises, agent-based; PCs and servers via client + Group Policy
  • Cloud-native MDM/MAM; over the internet, no on-prem infrastructure
  • Both tools manage Windows jointly; workloads shift gradually to Intune
  • Platforms
  • Windows and Windows Server; no native mobile OS management
  • Windows, macOS, iOS/iPadOS, Android, Linux : full cross-platform
  • Windows 10/11 only; needs ConfigMgr client + Intune enrollment
  • Software deployment
  • MSI, EXE, task sequences, OS imaging; deep control of complex installs
  • Store, Win32, LOB, and web apps via Company Portal
  • Either tool per workload : imaging in ConfigMgr, cloud apps in Intune
  • Key strengths
  • Rich inventory, OS deployment, full server management, proven at scale
  • No infrastructure, rapid deployment, Conditional Access, Autopilot, cross-platform
  • Best of both; migrate at your own pace while keeping ConfigMgr
  • Key limitations
  • Needs on-prem servers; no mobile OS management; complex to run
  • No OS imaging/task sequences; limited server mgmt; needs connectivity
  • More complex to configure; Windows only; still needs ConfigMgr during transition
  • Best for
  • Large enterprises with on-prem infrastructure and complex OS deployment
  • Cloud-first, remote/hybrid, BYOD, and mobile + desktop fleets
  • Organizations phasing from ConfigMgr to Intune without disruption
Slide 8

Understand the role of Microsoft Entra ID in Intune

  • Microsoft Entra ID
  • The cloud identity foundation for Intune : every policy is identity-based
  • Entra registered
  • Lightweight relationship for BYOD
  • Light management via app protection policies
  • Windows, iOS, and Android
  • Best for: personal devices
  • Entra joined
  • Cloud-only corporate devices
  • Full Intune (MDM) management
  • Smoothest OOBE; supports Autopilot
  • Best for: cloud-first Windows
  • Hybrid Entra joined
  • On-premises AD + cloud identity
  • Enables co-management scenarios
  • Requires Entra Connect
  • Best for: on-prem dependency
  • Without Entra ID, Intune can't function : all operations depend on verified identities. Policies, apps, and compliance are all assigned by identity.
Slide 9

Microsoft Entra ID : components and how integration works

  • Key components
  • User identities : enrollment and authentication
  • Group identities : organize users/devices for assignment
  • Device identities : track device info and compliance
  • Authentication tokens : verify access and device state
  • Conditional Access : enforce security by identity + compliance
  • How integration works
  • 1 Authenticate
  • Device enrollment starts with Entra ID sign-in
  • 2 Register or join
  • Device object is created for Intune to reference
  • 3 Assign via groups
  • Policies target Entra groups
  • 4 Conditional Access
  • Compliance enforced before resource access
  • Example : a clinician opens patient records: Entra ID verifies identity and device compliance before Intune-managed access is granted.
Slide 10

Evaluate device management models

  • Cloud-only
  • Devices join Entra ID directly : no on-prem AD
  • Intune handles MDM + MAM; Conditional Access enforces security
  • SSO to cloud apps, MFA, modern provisioning
  • Best for: cloud-first, remote, BYOD
  • Hybrid
  • On-prem Active Directory + Entra ID (hybrid join)
  • Group Policy for some settings, Intune for modern management
  • Balances legacy apps with cloud migration
  • Best for: existing AD investments
  • Co-managed
  • Configuration Manager + Intune manage the device together
  • ConfigMgr for traditional tasks, Intune for modern features
  • Workloads shift gradually to Intune
  • Best for: ConfigMgr-heavy estates
  • Choosing the right model
  • Base the decision on existing infrastructure, migration goals, and device requirements : cloud-only for simplicity and modern features, hybrid for on-premises dependencies, co-managed for a gradual ConfigMgr-to-Intune transition. Consider device types, user locations, security needs, and app dependencies.
Slide 11

Explore mobile device management (MDM) capabilities

  • MDM controls and protects devices over the internet through an MDM provider such as Microsoft Intune : no physical access required.
  • Provision
  • Configure
  • Secure
  • Monitor
  • Retire
  • Core capabilities
  • Enrollment : zero-touch via Autopilot, ADE, Android Enterprise
  • Configuration : profiles for Wi-Fi, VPN, email, certificates
  • Security : baselines and compliance (encryption, PIN, OS)
  • Apps & remote actions
  • Deploy store, LOB, and web apps to targeted groups
  • Monitor installation status and app versions centrally
  • Remote lock, wipe, reset, passcode removal, restart
  • Benefits
  • 100% cloud : no on-prem infrastructure
  • One console across Windows, iOS, Android, macOS
  • Automated compliance + Conditional Access
  • Self-service enrollment via Company Portal; scales easily

Kenniscontrole

Kies per vraag één antwoord. Je kunt opnieuw proberen; de score bepaalt je modulevoortgang niet.

1. Je wilt bestaande Windows-clients geleidelijk vanuit de cloud beheren. Wat past daarbij?

2. Wat past bij bescherming van bedrijfsdata in ondersteunde apps op een persoonlijk toestel?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 12 · Question 1

Contoso has 3,000 Windows devices currently managed by Configuration Manager. Leadership wants to start managing mobile devices and enable Conditional Access without replacing their existing on-premises infrastructure. Which management approach best fits this scenario?

  1. Cloud-only management with Intune
  2. Co-management with Configuration Manager and Intune
  3. Hybrid identity with on-premises MDM only
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: B. Co-management with Configuration Manager and Intune Correct. Co-management lets Contoso keep Configuration Manager for existing Windows devices while gradually extending Intune capabilities. Mobile devices and Conditional Access can be enabled immediately without replacing the on-premises infrastructure. A. Incorrect. Cloud-only management would require migrating away from Configuration Manager entirely. Contoso wants to keep their existing on-premises infrastructure while adding cloud capabilities. C. Incorrect. An on-premises MDM doesn't provide the cloud-based Conditional Access or mobile device support Contoso needs. Co-management with Intune is the right path.

Slide 12 · Question 2

A user at Fabrikam signs in to their corporate laptop with their Microsoft Entra account. Intune pushes a security configuration profile to the device. What is Microsoft Entra ID's role in this process?

  1. It stores the configuration profile settings and delivers them to the device
  2. It verifies the user's identity and confirms the device is registered, enabling Intune to target the correct device
  3. It encrypts the configuration profile during transit to the device
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: B. It verifies the user's identity and confirms the device is registered, enabling Intune to target the correct device Correct. Microsoft Entra ID provides the identity infrastructure that Intune depends on. It authenticates the user, holds the device object, and enables Intune to associate policies with the right user and device identity. A. Incorrect. Intune stores and delivers configuration profiles. Microsoft Entra ID is responsible for identity : verifying who the user is and whether the device is authorized to receive policies. C. Incorrect. Microsoft Entra ID handles identity and authorization, not profile encryption. Intune manages the secure delivery of configuration policies to enrolled devices.

Slide 12 · Question 3

Northwind Traders is a new company with no on-premises infrastructure. They want full control over device configuration, app deployment, and compliance enforcement for Windows, iOS, and Android devices from day one. Which management model should they use?

  1. Hybrid management
  2. Co-management
  3. Cloud-only management with Microsoft Intune
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: C. Cloud-only management with Microsoft Intune Correct. Cloud-only management with Intune is the ideal fit for a new organization with no on-premises infrastructure. It provides full MDM capabilities across Windows, iOS, and Android from a single cloud-based console without requiring any on-premises components. A. Incorrect. Hybrid management requires on-premises Active Directory infrastructure, which Northwind Traders doesn't have. It's designed for organizations transitioning from on-premises to cloud, not for new cloud-first organizations. B. Incorrect. Co-management requires Configuration Manager to already be in place managing Windows devices. Northwind Traders has no existing on-premises management infrastructure to co-manage with.

Slide 13 · Question 1

An administrator needs to remotely wipe a lost Android device that contains sensitive corporate data. The device is enrolled in Microsoft Intune. Which Intune MDM capability enables this action?

  1. Device configuration profiles
  2. Remote device actions
  3. Compliance policies
Answer and source explanation

Q4 - Correct: B. Remote device actions Correct. Intune's MDM capabilities include remote device actions such as remote wipe, retire, lock, and reset passcode. These actions can be triggered from the Intune admin center for any enrolled device, regardless of its current location. A. Incorrect. Configuration profiles push settings to devices but don't perform remote actions. Remote wipe is an MDM remote action that can be triggered on-demand from the Intune admin center. C. Incorrect. Compliance policies assess whether a device meets defined security requirements and can mark devices as noncompliant. They don't directly wipe a device : that requires a remote device action.

Slide 13 · Question 2

Tailspin Toys uses Configuration Manager to deploy software to 500 on-premises Windows devices. They want to enable Intune policies for those same devices without disrupting existing software deployments. What should they configure?

  1. Migrate all devices to Intune-only management immediately
  2. Enable co-management and keep the software deployment workload in Configuration Manager
  3. Deploy a second MDM server on-premises alongside Configuration Manager
Answer and source explanation

Q5 - Correct: B. Enable co-management and keep the software deployment workload in Configuration Manager Correct. Co-management lets you split workloads between Configuration Manager and Intune. Tailspin Toys can enable co-management to start using Intune features while leaving the software deployment workload with Configuration Manager, preserving existing deployment workflows. A. Incorrect. An immediate migration would disrupt existing Configuration Manager software deployments. Co-management allows Tailspin Toys to selectively move workloads to Intune while keeping software deployment in Configuration Manager. C. Incorrect. Adding a second on-premises MDM server would create management complexity and doesn't leverage the cloud-based capabilities of Intune. Co-management is the supported path for organizations already using Configuration Manager.

Learning Path 01Module 02

Entra ID configureren voor apparaat- en beleidsbeheer

Je richt gebruikers, groepen en administratieve bevoegdheden in als basis voor betrouwbaar endpointbeheer.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Onjuiste licenties, groepslidmaatschappen of rollen kunnen inschrijving blokkeren of beleid bij een verkeerde doelgroep afleveren.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: gebruikers en licenties.
  • Uitleggen: groepen en dynamisch lidmaatschap.
  • Uitleggen: rollen en minimale bevoegdheden.
  • Uitleggen: registratie, join en filters.

01. Gebruikers en licenties

Een gebruiker heeft een herkenbare UPN en de juiste licenties nodig. Controleer de usage location bij licentietoewijzing. Houd gebruikersobject, apparaatobject en Intune-inschrijving uit elkaar: een geldige aanmelding bewijst niet dat een toestel ook beheerd wordt.

02. Groepen en dynamisch lidmaatschap

Beveiligingsgroepen vormen doelgroepen voor apps en beleid. Statische groepen bieden directe controle; dynamische groepen evalueren kenmerken volgens een regel. Wijzigingen worden niet noodzakelijk onmiddellijk verwerkt. Controleer de regel, de bronattributen en het effectieve lidmaatschap voordat je een toewijzingsprobleem onderzoekt.

03. Rollen en minimale bevoegdheden

Gebruik de smalste rol die de taak ondersteunt. Een brede Entra-rol zoals Intune Administrator is niet geschikt als standaard voor elk helpdeskaccount. Scheid gebruikersbeheer, beleidsbeheer en ondersteuning. Controleer ook bestaande roltoewijzingen: meerdere rollen kunnen samen meer toegang geven dan bedoeld.

04. Registratie, join en filters

Bepaal wie apparaten mag registreren of joinen en welke limieten gelden. Groepen bepalen de doelgroep; toewijzingsfilters verfijnen ondersteunde opdrachten op apparaatkenmerken. Een filter is geen vervanging voor een gebruikersgroep en verleent geen administratieve rechten. Test de uiteindelijke doelgroep vóór productie.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Controleer licentie en effectieve groepsleden.
  • Gebruik rollen met minimale rechten.
  • Maak onderscheid tussen groepslidmaatschap en toewijzingsfilters.

Samenvatting

Controleer licentie en effectieve groepsleden. Gebruik rollen met minimale rechten. Maak onderscheid tussen groepslidmaatschap en toewijzingsfilters. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Cursusverdieping per onderwerp (Engels) · 5 bronslides

Original course content. Text order follows the source presentation; visual tables and diagrams are represented as text.

Slide 16

Understand the role of Microsoft Entra ID in endpoint management

  • Manages device identity
  • Three sign-up options: Entra registered, Entra joined, Entra hybrid joined
  • Enables group management of devices
  • Validates enrollment
  • Checks the device meets the requirements before it can enroll in Intune
  • Conditional Access
  • Evaluates compliance status, risk level, location, and device identity before access
  • Single sign-on
  • Extends the single sign-on experience across cloud resources
Slide 17

Create and Manage Users and Groups

  • Manage users
  • Required properties
  • User principal name (UPN): user@domain.com
  • Display name; usage location (required for licensing)
  • Bulk operations : CSV upload to create, invite, or delete
  • Lifecycle management
  • Onboarding → assign licenses + groups
  • Role changes → update memberships
  • Offboarding → disable / delete accounts
  • Use Microsoft Entra lifecycle workflows to automate onboarding and offboarding.
  • Work with groups
  • Group type
  • Members
  • Use for Intune?
  • Security group
  • Users, devices, groups
  • Policies & profiles
  • Microsoft 365 group
  • Users only
  • Apps only : not device policies
  • Group assignment
  • How it works
  • Assigned
  • Manual add / remove
  • Dynamic User
  • Rules based on user attributes
  • Dynamic Device
  • Rules based on device attributes
Slide 18

Assign Microsoft Entra ID Roles for Device Management

  • Role
  • Primary permissions
  • Intune Administrator
  • Full Intune management (privileged : avoid for daily use)
  • Cloud Device Administrator
  • Enable/disable/delete devices; read BitLocker keys
  • Groups Administrator
  • Create and manage security and M365 groups
  • User Administrator
  • Create users/groups, reset passwords, assign licenses
  • Helpdesk Administrator
  • Reset passwords, read BitLocker keys, force sign-out
  • Principle of least privilege
  • Avoid Global Administrator for routine device management
  • Avoid Intune Administrator for daily tasks : use Intune's built-in RBAC roles
  • Policy & Profile Manager, Endpoint Security Manager, or Help Desk Operator
  • Scoped administration
  • Administrative Units restrict a role to a subset of the org (e.g., regional offices)
  • Time-bound assignments via Privileged Identity Management (PIM) : requires Entra ID P2
Slide 19

Configure Device Registration Settings

  • Setting
  • Options
  • Recommended
  • Users may register devices
  • All / Selected / None
  • Selected (BYOD control)
  • Users may join devices to Entra
  • All / Selected / None
  • All or Selected
  • Max devices per user
  • 1-100 or Unlimited
  • 10-15
  • Require MFA to register/join
  • Yes / No
  • Use Conditional Access instead
  • Local admins (Entra-joined)
  • Global Admins get local admin automatically
  • Add security groups for IT support staff only
  • Microsoft Entra LAPS
  • Stores and rotates the built-in local admin password
  • Enable the toggle, then configure a LAPS policy in Intune (Account protection)
  • Restrict BitLocker recovery
  • Prevents standard users from self-recovering keys
  • Requires at least Privileged Role Administrator to change
  • Prefer a Conditional Access policy targeting “Register or join devices” over the legacy MFA toggle.
Slide 20

Dynamic Group Membership and Filters

  • Rule structure
  • <property> <operator> <value>
  • user.department -eq "Marketing"
  • device.deviceOSType -ne "Windows"
  • Combine conditions
  • (user.department -eq "Sales") -and (user.country -eq "US")
  • (user.department -eq "Sales") -or (user.department -eq "Marketing")
  • Common property
  • Type
  • Example rule
  • department
  • User
  • user.department -eq "Sales"
  • jobTitle
  • User
  • user.jobTitle -contains "Manager"
  • usageLocation
  • User
  • user.usageLocation -eq "US"
  • deviceOSType
  • Device
  • device.deviceOSType -eq "Windows"
  • deviceOwnership
  • Device
  • device.deviceOwnership -eq "Company"
  • enrollmentProfileName
  • Device
  • device.enrollmentProfileName -eq "Autopilot"
  • deviceOSVersion
  • Device
  • device.deviceOSVersion -startsWith "10.0"

Kenniscontrole

Kies per vraag één antwoord. Je kunt opnieuw proberen; de score bepaalt je modulevoortgang niet.

1. Een gebruiker verandert van afdeling. Wat bepaalt het nieuwe lidmaatschap van een dynamische afdelingsgroep?

2. Hoe verfijn je een ondersteunde beleidstoewijzing op apparaatmodel?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 21 · Question 1

At Contoso Healthcare, clinical coordinator Maria is moving from Cardiology to Emergency Medicine. You created a dynamic group named 'Emergency-Devices' with the rule (user.department -eq "Emergency Medicine") to assign compliance policies. What happens to Maria's enrolled devices after her department attribute is updated in Microsoft Entra ID?

  1. The devices remain in the Cardiology group until manually moved by an administrator
  2. The devices are automatically added to the Emergency-Devices group during the next membership evaluation
  3. Maria must re-enroll her devices to join the new department's device group
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: B. The devices are automatically added to the Emergency-Devices group during the next membership evaluation Correct. Dynamic groups automatically re-evaluate membership when user or device attributes change. After Maria's department attribute is updated, her devices are added to the group during the next evaluation cycle. This automation eliminates manual group management. A. Incorrect. Dynamic groups evaluate membership automatically based on attribute changes. Manual intervention isn't required. C. Incorrect. Device re-enrollment isn't required when dynamic group membership changes. Membership is evaluated based on current attributes.

Slide 21 · Question 2

You're configuring role-based access control (RBAC) at Contoso Healthcare. The Helpdesk team needs to remotely wipe lost devices, view device inventory, and disable noncompliant devices. They should NOT be able to create or modify Intune policies. Which role should you assign to grant only these permissions?

  1. Intune Administrator
  2. Cloud Device Administrator
  3. Helpdesk Operator
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: C. Helpdesk Operator Correct. The Helpdesk Operator role (an Intune built-in RBAC role, not a Microsoft Entra ID role) is designed for support staff who need to perform device management tasks like remote wipe, restart, and disable devices without permissions to modify policies. This follows the principle of least privilege. A. Incorrect. The Intune Administrator role has full permissions to manage all Intune aspects, including creating and modifying policies. This exceeds the Helpdesk team's required permissions. B. Incorrect. The Cloud Device Administrator can manage device objects in Microsoft Entra ID, but lacks permissions for Intune actions like remote wipe or viewing the Intune device inventory.

Slide 21 · Question 3

Contoso Healthcare implemented a BYOD program for administrative staff. You need to allow personal iOS and Android devices to register in Microsoft Entra ID, but require multifactor authentication (MFA) during registration. Where should you configure this requirement?

  1. In Device settings under the 'Devices may be Microsoft Entra joined' option
  2. In User settings under 'Users may register their devices with Microsoft Entra ID'
  3. In Conditional Access policies by creating a policy that requires MFA for device registration
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: C. In Conditional Access policies by creating a policy that requires MFA for device registration Correct. To require MFA during device registration, create a Conditional Access policy that targets the 'Device registration' cloud app. This approach provides granular control over authentication requirements for registration activities. A. Incorrect. The 'Devices may be Microsoft Entra joined' setting controls whether devices can be joined (for Windows devices), not registered. MFA requirements are configured separately. B. Incorrect. The 'Users may register their devices' setting controls who can register devices, but doesn't provide options for requiring MFA. This setting only enables or disables registration.

Slide 22 · Question 1

Contoso Healthcare is purchasing 100 new Windows 11 Pro laptops for nursing staff. These corporate-owned devices need access to Azure resources and full Intune management. Your organization doesn't have on-premises Active Directory. Which device identity approach should you implement?

  1. Microsoft Entra hybrid join
  2. Microsoft Entra join
  3. Microsoft Entra registration
Answer and source explanation

Q4 - Correct: B. Microsoft Entra join Correct. Microsoft Entra join is the optimal choice for cloud-first organizations without on-premises Active Directory. It establishes device identity directly in Microsoft Entra ID, enables single sign-on to cloud resources, and provides full management through Intune. A. Incorrect. Microsoft Entra hybrid join requires on-premises Active Directory Domain Services (AD DS). Since Contoso Healthcare doesn't have on-premises AD DS, this option isn't viable. C. Incorrect. Microsoft Entra registration is designed for BYOD and personal device scenarios. For corporate-owned devices that need full management, Microsoft Entra join is the appropriate choice.

Slide 22 · Question 2

You need to create groups at Contoso Healthcare to target Intune compliance policies and configuration profiles. You have clinical staff with shared workstations, administrative staff with personal laptops, and IT staff with corporate devices. Which combination of group types best supports efficient policy targeting?

  1. Create three static user groups for each department and manually assign members
  2. Create dynamic user groups based on department attributes and dynamic device groups based on device ownership (corporate vs. personal)
  3. Create static device groups and assign devices manually based on their physical location
Answer and source explanation

Q5 - Correct: B. Create dynamic user groups based on department attributes and dynamic device groups based on device ownership (corporate vs. personal) Correct. Dynamic user groups automatically organize users by attributes like department, while dynamic device groups distinguish corporate-owned from personal devices. This combination enables accurate policy targeting without manual management and ensures memberships update automatically as roles change or new devices enroll. A. Incorrect. Static groups require manual maintenance as staff join, leave, or change roles. This approach increases administrative overhead in a 500-employee organization. C. Incorrect. Managing groups by physical location requires continuous manual updates and doesn't align with cloud-first management. Device location changes frequently in healthcare, making this approach inefficient.

Learning Path 01Module 03

Apparaatidentiteit en authenticatie beheren

Je vergelijkt apparaatregistratie, Entra join en hybrid join en onderzoekt problemen met apparaatvertrouwen.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Toegang kan mislukken terwijl een apparaat zichtbaar is in een portal. De juiste joinstatus en tokens zijn noodzakelijk voor betrouwbare authenticatie.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: drie relaties met Entra ID.
  • Uitleggen: windows Hello for Business.
  • Uitleggen: join en automatische inschrijving.
  • Uitleggen: diagnostiek van vertrouwen.

01. Drie relaties met Entra ID

Entra registered past vaak bij persoonlijke apparaten die een werkaccount gebruiken. Entra joined past bij zakelijke, cloudgerichte Windows-apparaten. Hybrid joined combineert een lokaal AD-domeinlidmaatschap met een cloudidentiteit. Kies op basis van de aanmeldingswijze, bestaande afhankelijkheden en gewenste beheerstrategie.

02. Windows Hello for Business

Windows Hello for Business gebruikt een aan het apparaat gebonden sleutel in plaats van telkens een wachtwoord te versturen. TPM-bescherming helpt sleutels te beveiligen. In hybride omgevingen onderscheid je cloud Kerberos trust, key trust en certificate trust. Vergelijk PKI-afhankelijkheden en de actuele vereisten voordat je een model kiest.

03. Join en automatische inschrijving

Entra join en Intune-inschrijving zijn verschillende stappen. Voor automatische MDM-inschrijving moeten onder meer licenties, gebruikersscope en inschrijvingsbeperkingen kloppen. Controleer daarna zowel het Entra-apparaatobject als de beheerde apparaatregistratie in Intune. Een gelukt aanmeldvenster is onvoldoende bewijs van beheer.

04. Diagnostiek van vertrouwen

Gebruik dsregcmd /status om onder meer AzureAdJoined, DomainJoined en AzureAdPrt te onderzoeken. Lees de uitvoer in de juiste gebruikerscontext. Controleer tijd, netwerk, gebruikersstatus en apparaatobjecten. Combineer dit met User Device Registration-events en Entra-aanmeldlogboeken om de foutfase vast te stellen.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Registratie, join en MDM-inschrijving zijn afzonderlijke begrippen.
  • Een PRT ondersteunt SSO naar cloudresources.
  • Onderzoek tokens en logs voordat je opnieuw joint.

Samenvatting

Registratie, join en MDM-inschrijving zijn afzonderlijke begrippen. Een PRT ondersteunt SSO naar cloudresources. Onderzoek tokens en logs voordat je opnieuw joint. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Slide 26

Use device identities well

  • How device identities are used
  • Conditional Access : evaluate join type, managed state, and compliance before granting access
  • Intune targeting : assign profiles, policies, and apps using device attributes and groups
  • Inventory and reporting : identify unsupported OS versions and unmanaged or risky devices
  • Design principles
  • Prefer Entra join for corporate Windows devices authenticating directly to Entra ID
  • Block personal Windows device enrollment for granular control of the Windows fleet
  • Use Hybrid Entra join where an on-prem dependency plus cloud reporting is needed
  • Keep device names and tags consistent for group rules and filters
  • Automate retire/wipe for lost or decommissioned devices

Slide 28

Authentication Methods : Cloud Kerberos, Key Trust, Certificate Trust, and TPM

  • Trust models
  • Trust model
  • PKI required?
  • Recommended for
  • Cloud Kerberos
  • No
  • New hybrid deployments : Microsoft recommended
  • Key trust
  • DC certificates only
  • Existing PKI, no user certs needed
  • Certificate trust
  • DC + user certs
  • Full PKI, user cert requirement
  • The role of TPM
  • TPM 2.0 : required for modern Windows; hardware-backed key protection
  • Private key never leaves the hardware : stronger Conditional Access signal
  • Windows 11 requires TPM 2.0
  • Without TPM: software keystores : less secure, weaker compliance signal
  • Trust types apply to hybrid deployments only : cloud-only deployments don't require a trust type.
Slide 29

Choose a Windows Hello for Business trust model

  • → Cloud-only tenant?
  • No trust type needed
  • → Hybrid : new deployment?
  • Start with Cloud Kerberos trust (no PKI, recommended)
  • → Hybrid : existing PKI, no user certs?
  • Key trust
  • → Hybrid : existing PKI + user certs required?
  • Certificate trust
  • Key principle
  • Require TPM in production deployments to prevent credential theft
  • Strengthens Conditional Access device signals
Slide 30

Configure Device Trust and Join Settings

  • Scenario 1 : Entra Join + automatic Intune enrollment
  • Intune admin center → Devices > Enrollment > Windows > Automatic Enrollment
  • Set MDM user scope to target groups
  • During OOBE: Set up for work or school → sign in with Entra credentials
  • Device joins Entra ID and Intune enrollment runs automatically
  • Verify: dsregcmd /status → AzureAdJoined: YES
  • For new deployments, evaluate Windows Autopilot device preparation : no hardware-hash pre-registration.
  • Scenario 2 : Hybrid Join via Entra Connect
  • Install Entra Connect with device writeback and hybrid join enabled
  • Configure device OU selection (if filtering by OU)
  • Ensure devices reach required endpoints; verify service-account permissions
  • Restart or force sync : domain-joined devices register with Entra ID
  • Verify: dsregcmd /status → DomainJoined: YES and AzureAdJoined: YES
  • Entra Cloud Sync does not support device sync for hybrid join : Entra Connect is required.
Slide 31

Validate and Troubleshoot Device Trust Issues

  • Troubleshooting checklist
  • Check join type and enrollment status in the Entra admin center
  • Run dsregcmd /status : inspect join state and last errors
  • Verify Intune enrollment status in the Intune admin center
  • Check Entra Connect sync health (hybrid only)
  • Review device event logs for specific error codes
  • ⚠ dsregcmd /leave immediately unenrolls the device : use only for controlled troubleshooting.
  • Key dsregcmd /status fields
  • Field
  • Expected value
  • AzureAdJoined
  • YES (Entra joined or hybrid)
  • DomainJoined
  • YES (hybrid only)
  • AzureAdPrt
  • YES (SSO Primary Refresh Token present)
  • WorkplaceJoined
  • YES (registered / BYOD)
Slide 32

Troubleshoot device trust : logs and common symptoms

  • Log sources
  • Event Viewer : Applications and Services > Microsoft > Windows > User Device Registration
  • Intune diagnostics : export from the Intune admin center
  • Network : confirm access to login.microsoftonline.com and enterpriseregistration.windows.net
  • Symptom
  • Likely cause
  • Fix
  • Registered, not managed
  • User registered but didn't enroll
  • Confirm MDM auto-enrollment scope; provide enrollment steps
  • Hybrid devices missing in Entra
  • Entra Connect or wrong OU
  • Verify options and OU; run sync; check errors
  • WHfB key/cert errors
  • No TPM, policy mismatch, or PKI issue
  • Confirm TPM; review WHfB policy; validate PKI
  • AzureAdPrt: NO (no SSO/PRT)
  • Registration or connectivity issue
  • Verify time sync + connectivity; re-register device

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 33 · Question 1

Your organization purchases new corporate Windows laptops that must be joined to Microsoft Entra ID and automatically managed by Microsoft Intune during initial setup. Which provisioning approach meets this requirement with the least administrative overhead?

  1. Use Microsoft Entra join with automatic MDM enrollment so devices join Microsoft Entra ID during setup and enroll in Intune automatically.
  2. Use Microsoft Entra hybrid join and manual Intune enrollment after provisioning to keep devices domain-joined and managed.
  3. Use Microsoft Entra ID registration (workplace join) and rely on users to install the Company Portal to enroll devices.
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: A. Use Microsoft Entra join with automatic MDM enrollment so devices join Microsoft Entra ID during setup and enroll in Intune automatically. Microsoft Entra join combined with automatic MDM enrollment allows devices to join Microsoft Entra ID and enroll into Intune during OOBE by signing in with an organizational account. B. Hybrid join makes devices domain-joined and synced to Microsoft Entra ID but requires extra AD and Microsoft Entra Connect configuration. It doesn't provide automatic Intune enrollment during OOBE by default. C. Microsoft Entra ID registration is intended for personal devices and requires user action to enroll. It doesn't meet the automatic corporate provisioning requirement.

Slide 33 · Question 2

You run dsregcmd /status on a user's device and see: AzureAdJoined: YES, DomainJoined: NO, DeviceId: a GUID, and WorkplaceJoined: NO. The user can't access an internal app protected by a policy requiring Microsoft Entra hybrid joined devices. What is the most likely cause and next step?

  1. The device is Microsoft Entra joined but not hybrid joined. Update the Conditional Access policy to accept Microsoft Entra joined devices.
  2. The device isn't registered with Microsoft Entra ID. Run dsregcmd /join to register it immediately.
  3. The device lacks the Company Portal and can't meet device compliance. Ask the user to install the app.
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: A. The device is Microsoft Entra joined but not hybrid joined. Update the Conditional Access policy to accept Microsoft Entra joined devices. AzureAdJoined: YES indicates Microsoft Entra joined status, while DomainJoined: NO means it isn't domain-joined or hybrid joined. Either the device must be hybrid-joined or the policy must be adjusted. B. The device shows AzureAdJoined: YES and a DeviceId, so it's already registered. Running dsregcmd /join is unnecessary for this symptom. C. Company Portal affects Intune enrollment and compliance, but the immediate issue is that the device isn't domain-joined or hybrid joined as the policy requires.

Slide 33 · Question 3

Several on-premises domain-joined laptops aren't appearing as Microsoft Entra hybrid joined after configuring hybrid join with Microsoft Entra Connect. Which configuration issue is most likely preventing hybrid registration?

  1. The Microsoft Entra Connect service connection point (SCP) is missing or misconfigured in on-premises AD, so devices can't discover the hybrid join configuration.
  2. Intune auto-enrollment is disabled in Microsoft Entra ID, preventing devices from becoming hybrid joined.
  3. Users turned off Windows Hello for Business, which is required for hybrid registration.
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: A. The Microsoft Entra Connect service connection point (SCP) is missing or misconfigured in on-premises AD, so devices can't discover the hybrid join configuration. Microsoft Entra hybrid join depends on an SCP in on-premises AD (created by Microsoft Entra Connect) so devices can find the tenant registration configuration. A missing or misconfigured SCP prevents hybrid registration. B. Intune auto-enrollment affects MDM enrollment, not the hybrid join process. Hybrid join depends on Microsoft Entra Connect and device SCP discovery. C. Windows Hello for Business isn't a prerequisite for Microsoft Entra hybrid join. Hybrid registration requires SCP, Microsoft Entra Connect sync, and network reachability to Azure endpoints.

Slide 34 · Question 1

A helpdesk receives a ticket: a user's device shows AzureAdPrt: NO in dsregcmd /status and the user can't access cloud resources using a primary refresh token (PRT). Which troubleshooting step is most appropriate to resolve device-based SSO?

  1. Verify the device has valid time sync and connectivity to Microsoft Entra ID endpoints. If needed, re-run dsregcmd /join or re-register the device.
  2. Immediately disable the user's account in Microsoft Entra ID and recreate it to force token issuance.
  3. Uninstall the Company Portal to force a new enrollment, which recreates the PRT automatically.
Answer and source explanation

Q4 - Correct: A. Verify the device has valid time sync and connectivity to Microsoft Entra ID endpoints. If needed, re-run dsregcmd /join or re-register the device. PRT acquisition requires correct device registration, time sync, and connectivity to Microsoft Entra ID. Re-registering the device or fixing connectivity often restores the PRT and SSO. B. Disabling and recreating accounts is disruptive and unrelated to device PRT issues. Troubleshooting should target device registration and connectivity first. C. Company Portal relates to Intune enrollment. PRT results from device and user authentication with Microsoft Entra ID and isn't fixed by removing Company Portal.

Slide 34 · Question 2

You need to create a dynamic device group in Intune to target a compliance policy to only corporate-owned Windows 11 devices that are Microsoft Entra joined. Which combination of attributes should you use in the dynamic group query?

  1. Filter by deviceOwnership equals 'Company', operatingSystem equals 'Windows', and trustType equals 'AzureAD'.
  2. Filter by managedBy equals 'Intune' and deviceName contains 'WIN' to identify Windows devices.
  3. Filter by isManaged equals 'true' and operatingSystemVersion contains '11' to target Windows 11 devices.
Answer and source explanation

Q5 - Correct: A. Filter by deviceOwnership equals 'Company', operatingSystem equals 'Windows', and trustType equals 'AzureAD'. These three attributes accurately identify corporate-owned Windows devices that are Microsoft Entra joined. deviceOwnership distinguishes corporate from personal, operatingSystem identifies Windows, and trustType 'AzureAD' indicates Microsoft Entra joined. B. managedBy indicates enrollment status but doesn't specify join type or ownership. Device names aren't reliable filters since naming conventions vary. C. This might capture managed Windows 11 devices, but it doesn't distinguish between Microsoft Entra joined and hybrid joined devices, nor does it filter by corporate ownership.

Slide 34 · Question 3

Your organization deploys Windows Hello for Business to 500 new Windows 11 laptops for remote workers. There's no on-premises Active Directory and the security team requires hardware-backed credential storage. Which configuration should you implement?

  1. Deploy Windows Hello for Business with TPM 2.0 required. Cloud-only deployments don't use a trust type; TPM 2.0 provides the hardware-backed key storage.
  2. Deploy certificate trust with an on-premises certificate authority to issue device certificates for authentication.
  3. Deploy key trust without TPM requirements since Windows 11 stores keys securely in software-based keystores.
Answer and source explanation

Q6 - Correct: A. Deploy Windows Hello for Business with TPM 2.0 required. Cloud-only deployments don't use a trust type; TPM 2.0 provides the hardware-backed key storage. Correct. Key trust and certificate trust apply only to hybrid deployments that authenticate to on-premises Active Directory. In a cloud-only environment (no on-premises AD), Windows Hello for Business requires no trust type : you deploy WHfB and require TPM 2.0 for hardware-backed credential storage. B. Incorrect. Certificate trust requires an on-premises certificate authority and is a hybrid model. This organization is cloud-only with no on-premises AD or PKI. C. Incorrect. Key trust is a hybrid-only model that requires domain-controller certificates from a PKI, and software-based keystores don't meet the hardware-backed storage requirement. TPM 2.0 must be required.

Learning Path 01Module 04

Apparaatinschrijving plannen en uitvoeren

Je kiest passende inschrijvingsmethoden voor Windows, Apple en Android en bewaakt de instroom.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

De gekozen inschrijving bepaalt privacy, beheerfuncties en de mogelijkheden om apparaten later veilig uit dienst te nemen.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: een inschrijvingsstrategie kiezen.
  • Uitleggen: windows en Apple.
  • Uitleggen: android Enterprise.
  • Uitleggen: inschrijvingsfouten afbakenen.

01. Een inschrijvingsstrategie kiezen

Leg per doelgroep platform, eigendom en gewenste beheerrechten vast. Bevestig licenties, ondersteunde OS-versies en netwerktoegang. Configureer beperkingen voor persoonlijke apparaten en aantallen. Verwar een Entra-apparaatlimiet niet met de Intune-inschrijvingslimiet: zij gelden op verschillende momenten in de keten.

02. Windows en Apple

Windows kent onder meer automatische MDM-inschrijving, Autopilot en Company Portal. Apple Automated Device Enrollment gebruikt Apple Business Manager of School Manager voor zakelijke apparaten. Controleer het APNs-certificaat, enrollment tokens en profieltoewijzing. Voor macOS bestaan ook alternatieve routes; kies bewust tussen gebruikersaffiniteit en gedeeld gebruik.

03. Android Enterprise

Koppel Managed Google Play en kies een Android Enterprise-model. Een persoonlijk work profile scheidt werk en privé op BYOD. Fully managed richt zich op zakelijke toestellen; dedicated op een afgebakende taak. Corporate-owned work profile combineert zakelijk eigendom met een persoonlijke ruimte. Het eigendomsmodel bepaalt de beheergrenzen.

04. Inschrijvingsfouten afbakenen

Bepaal eerst of de fout bij authenticatie, registratie, platformintegratie of MDM-inschrijving ontstaat. Controleer licentie, scope, beperkingen en geldigheid van certificaten of tokens. Lees enrollment failures en clientlogs. Verwijder niet direct alle records: leg identificatoren en foutcodes vast zodat je de oorzaak kunt aantonen.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Kies de methode op platform én eigendom.
  • Bewaak Apple-certificaten en platformtokens.
  • Onderzoek de foutfase voordat je opnieuw inschrijft.

Samenvatting

Kies de methode op platform én eigendom. Bewaak Apple-certificaten en platformtokens. Onderzoek de foutfase voordat je opnieuw inschrijft. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Cursusverdieping per onderwerp (Engels) · 8 bronslides

Original course content. Text order follows the source presentation; visual tables and diagrams are represented as text.

Slide 37

Choose an enrollment strategy for your organization

  • Business scenario
  • Platform & enrollment method
  • Configuration & wipe action
  • BYOD : user-owned, needs work data
  • Android: work profile · iOS: User Enrollment · Win/Mac: Company Portal
  • Isolates work from personal data. Retire deletes only the corporate work profile.
  • Corporate-owned, single user
  • Android: Fully managed · iOS/Mac: ADE · Win: Autopilot
  • Full control, strict baselines, blocks unapproved apps. Wipe = full factory reset.
  • Corporate-owned, personally enabled (COPE)
  • Android: corporate work profile · iOS/Mac/Win: standard corporate enrollment
  • Full management with a separated secure profile. Wipe or selectively retire corporate data.
  • Dedicated, kiosk, or shared
  • Android: Dedicated · iOS: ADE (Supervised) · Win: Autopilot self-deploying
  • Single-app/kiosk, Managed Home Screen, restricted OS. Wipe to repurpose.
  • Specialized frontline (no Google services)
  • Android: Android Open Source Project (AOSP)
  • Custom settings and basic network profiles. Wipe or remove the management profile.
Slide 38

Enroll Windows devices using Microsoft Intune

  • Enrollment method
  • Best for
  • Requirements & notes
  • Automatic enrollment
  • Corporate + BYOD where users sign in with their work account; device joins Entra ID and Intune automatically
  • Entra ID P1/P2. Enable in the Intune admin center. Users add account via Settings → Access work or school.
  • Windows Autopilot
  • New corporate devices (user-driven, self-deploying, pre-provision, existing-device)
  • Register hardware IDs, assign a profile. Needs automatic enrollment + Entra ID P1/P2. Intune Connector for hybrid join.
  • User-driven enrollment
  • Existing corporate devices and BYOD without P1/P2
  • No P1/P2 required. Settings → Accounts → Access work or school → Connect.
  • Bulk enrollment
  • Large-scale, kiosk, or staged hardware
  • Provisioning package (Configuration Designer) at OOBE, or Device Enrollment Manager : up to 1,000 devices/account.
  • Co-management
  • Existing Configuration Manager estates moving to Intune gradually
  • Devices must be hybrid joined. Enable auto-enrollment from the ConfigMgr client; split workloads.
Slide 39

Enroll iOS/iPadOS devices using Microsoft Intune

  • Corporate-owned devices
  • Automated Device Enrollment (ADE)
  • Zero-touch, supervised by default (ABM/ASM). Ship to users : Setup Assistant runs preconfigured and enrolls automatically.
  • Auth: Setup Assistant with modern authentication : MFA, Entra registration, Conditional Access.
  • Apple Configurator
  • USB staging for devices not bought through ABM/ASM : connect to a Mac, apply the profile, supervise, hand off.
  • BYOD / personal devices
  • Account-driven User Enrollment
  • Faster BYOD onboarding, no Company Portal. Settings → VPN & Device Management; JIT registration with Microsoft Authenticator.
  • Company Portal app
  • Install from the App Store, sign in, then install the management profile from Settings.
  • Web-based enrollment
  • Open the enrollment portal in Safari, sign in, and install the profile : Authenticator deploys in the background.
Slide 40

Enroll macOS devices using Microsoft Intune

  • Recommended: Apple Automated Device Enrollment (ADE)
  • Zero-touch · supervised by default · ABM or ASM required. Ship Macs to users : Setup Assistant runs preconfigured and the device enrolls itself.
  • 1 Power on & Wi-Fi
  • Turn on, pick language/region, join Wi-Fi
  • 2 Migration Assistant
  • Transfer from Mac/Time Machine/PC, or set up fresh
  • 3 Remote Management
  • Setup Assistant: company can configure this Mac
  • 4 Sign in
  • User signs in with work account (or Apple ID per policy)
  • 5 Terms & diagnostics
  • Accept T&Cs; choose whether to share data with Apple
  • 6 Finish & ready
  • Optional FileVault, Touch ID, local account : enrolled
Slide 41

Enroll macOS : alternative paths

  • Apple Configurator + DEM
  • Corporate Macs not via ABM/ASM
  • USB-stage from another Mac, or assign a Device Enrollment Manager account for shared / lab hardware
  • Company Portal for macOS
  • BYOD / personal Macs
  • User installs the Intune Company Portal .pkg, signs in, and approves the management profile in System Settings
  • Supervised mode · UAMDM
  • Required for advanced payloads on macOS 11+
  • ADE auto-grants supervised / user-approved status : unlocking kernel extensions, update delays, and strict payloads
Slide 42

Enroll Android devices using Microsoft Intune

  • Enrollment model
  • Details
  • Personally owned work profile (BYOD)
  • Link Intune to Managed Google Play. User installs Company Portal, signs in; Android Enterprise creates an isolated work profile. IT manages only the work profile.
  • Corporate-owned, fully managed (COBO)
  • Business-only devices. Create a fully managed profile to generate a QR code; on a factory-reset device tap the welcome screen seven times and scan to enroll.
  • Corporate-owned dedicated (COSU)
  • Kiosks, scanners, signage. Same QR provisioning, but the device locks to a limited app set (kiosk / Managed Home Screen) and bypasses sign-in.
  • Corporate-owned work profile (COPE)
  • Company-owned devices employees may also use personally. Work-profile separation with broader IT control of the whole device.
  • Zero-touch (ZTE & Samsung KME)
  • Pre-register serial numbers so devices auto-enroll on first network connection. Google ZTE for non-Samsung, Samsung KME for Samsung.
  • Device administrator (DA) : deprecated
  • Legacy. Intune ended DA support on GMS devices (Aug 2024) : migrate to Android Enterprise. Non-GMS: use AOSP enrollment.
Slide 43

Configure enrollment restrictions in Microsoft Intune

  • A foundational compliance gate : define who can enroll which devices before Conditional Access or compliance policies apply. Two controls: device type (platforms allowed) and device limit (max devices per user).
  • Bring your own device (BYOD)
  • Ownership: personal · flexible access, light management
  • Methods
  • Entra ID device registration; Company Portal enrollment
  • Typical use
  • Email, Teams, M365 apps; Conditional Access with App Protection; light management
  • Recommended settings
  • Allow iOS/Android/Windows · limit 3-5 devices/user · restrict unsupported platforms · enforce minimum OS
  • Corporate-owned devices
  • Ownership: corporate · no private use (unless COPE)
  • Methods
  • Windows Autopilot; Apple ADE (via ABM); Android Enterprise (Fully Managed, Dedicated, COPE)
  • Usage profile
  • Strictly managed, standardized enrollment; fully owned for internal use
  • Recommended settings
  • Block personal platforms and unmanaged enrollments · enforce strict OS/security baselines · require full enrollment workflows
Slide 44

Troubleshoot device enrollment in Microsoft Intune

  • Separate management (Intune : policies, apps, compliance) from identity (Entra ID : sign-in, trust, lifecycle). Pick the right portal for the symptom.
  • Management layer : Intune admin center
  • All devices : check Last check-in (stale devices miss policies); use Sync; drill into Device compliance
  • Monitor > Enrollment failures : restriction, missing license, or platform mismatch
  • Overview dashboard : a compliance drop often means a new policy is causing conflicts
  • Identity layer : Entra admin center
  • All devices : a disabled device blocks all access; BitLocker keys live on the device object
  • Audit logs : trace which admin or rule deleted, disabled, or moved a device
  • Device settings : confirm “Users may register their devices” isn't set to None
  • Common fixes
  • Corporate device flagged as personal : register the hardware hash in Autopilot, sync the serial from ABM, or add a corporate device identifier.
  • OS version or platform blocked : Devices → Enrollment → platform restrictions → set Allow and clear/widen min/max versions.
  • Windows auto-enrollment fails : Entra → Mobility → Microsoft Intune: set MDM scope to All (or the right group) and MAM scope to None.

1. Welk Android-model past bij een persoonlijk toestel met gescheiden zakelijke apps?

2. Wat controleer je vroeg bij mislukte automatische Windows-inschrijving?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 45 · Question 1

Contoso wants to manage personal iOS devices used by physicians while protecting personal data and respecting user privacy. Which enrollment model best suits this requirement?

  1. Corporate-owned enrollment with full device wipe capabilities
  2. BYOD with Apple User Enrollment for a containerized work environment
  3. Supervised mode enrollment requiring device registration
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: B. BYOD with Apple User Enrollment for a containerized work environment Correct. Apple User Enrollment creates a mathematically separated volume on the device, allowing Intune to manage corporate apps and data while keeping personal data completely private. The work profile can be removed without affecting personal content. A. Incorrect. Corporate-owned enrollment grants the organization full control over the device, including the ability to wipe all data. This doesn't protect the user's personal data and is inappropriate for personal devices. C. Incorrect. Supervised mode provides the highest level of management control and is intended for corporate-owned devices. It does not protect personal data and is not appropriate for BYOD scenarios.

Slide 45 · Question 2

Contoso needs to enroll corporate-owned Windows devices with zero-touch provisioning and automatic configuration during initial setup. Which enrollment method should they implement?

  1. User-driven enrollment via Settings
  2. Automatic enrollment through Group Policy
  3. Windows Autopilot with deployment profiles
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: C. Windows Autopilot with deployment profiles Correct. Windows Autopilot uses device hardware IDs and deployment profiles to enable zero-touch provisioning. Devices automatically receive configuration during the out-of-the-box experience without IT intervention. A. Incorrect. User-driven enrollment via Settings requires manual steps from the user and IT staff. It does not provide zero-touch provisioning during the out-of-the-box experience. B. Incorrect. Group Policy-based enrollment requires devices to be domain-joined and does not enable zero-touch provisioning during initial device setup for cloud-managed scenarios.

Slide 45 · Question 3

Contoso's enrollment restrictions currently allow iOS 13 and later. A user attempts to enroll an iPad running iOS 12. What should happen?

  1. The device enrolls but receives a noncompliant warning
  2. The device is blocked from enrollment at the policy level
  3. The device enrolls automatically and compliance policies enforce restrictions
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: B. The device is blocked from enrollment at the policy level Correct. Enrollment restrictions apply before a device becomes managed. Platform version restrictions prevent unsupported devices from ever completing enrollment, acting as a foundational security gate. A. Incorrect. Enrollment restrictions apply before a device becomes managed. The device is blocked from enrolling entirely : it doesn't enroll and then become noncompliant. C. Incorrect. Compliance policies evaluate devices that are already enrolled. Enrollment restrictions prevent devices from enrolling in the first place when they don't meet platform version requirements.

Slide 46 · Question 1

An administrative staff member has successfully enrolled 3 corporate-owned Windows devices and tries to enroll a personal Windows tablet for BYOD access. The enrollment fails because device limits are reached. What policy controls this behavior?

  1. Conditional Access policy
  2. Enrollment device limit restriction
  3. Device Configuration policy
Answer and source explanation

Q4 - Correct: B. Enrollment device limit restriction Correct. Device limit restrictions control how many devices a user can enroll into the organization. These restrictions are part of enrollment restriction policies and apply across all enrollment scenarios regardless of device ownership. A. Incorrect. Conditional Access policies control access to resources based on conditions, not the number of devices a user can enroll into the organization. C. Incorrect. Device Configuration policies configure device settings after enrollment. They do not control the number of devices a user can enroll.

Slide 46 · Question 2

A clinical technician attempts to enroll their Android device but receives an 'Enrollment failed' error. The user has a valid Intune license and Android Enterprise enrollment is enabled. What is the most likely cause?

  1. The Android device doesn't support Android Enterprise
  2. Enrollment restrictions are blocking Android devices from enrolling
  3. The user has already enrolled the maximum number of allowed devices
Answer and source explanation

Q5 - Correct: B. Enrollment restrictions are blocking Android devices from enrolling Correct. When a user cannot enroll despite having a valid license and a supported platform, enrollment restrictions blocking that device type are the most common cause. Restrictions are evaluated before enrollment attempts proceed to configuration or compliance checks. A. Incorrect. While device compatibility is worth checking, the scenario states Android Enterprise enrollment is enabled and the user has a valid license. The more likely cause is a policy restriction rather than a platform limitation. C. Incorrect. While hitting a device limit would cause enrollment failure, the scenario doesn't indicate the user has existing enrolled devices. A blanket platform restriction is the more probable cause given no other details are provided.

Learning Path 01Module 05

Windows uitrollen met Autopilot

Je gebruikt Autopilot om de eerste ingebruikname van zakelijke Windows-apparaten te standaardiseren.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Een voorspelbare OOBE vermindert handwerk, maar vereist correcte registratie, profielen en een gecontroleerde appselectie.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: scenario’s en grenzen.
  • Uitleggen: registratie en profieltoewijzing.
  • Uitleggen: pre-provisioning en ESP.
  • Uitleggen: deployment onderzoeken.

01. Scenario’s en grenzen

Autopilot configureert de ingebruikname van een ondersteunde Windows-installatie. User-driven past bij een toegewezen gebruiker; self-deploying bij geschikte gedeelde apparaten of kiosks. Autopilot is geen algemene bare-metal imagingoplossing. Vergelijk daarnaast de actuele mogelijkheden van Windows Autopilot device preparation met de klassieke dienst.

02. Registratie en profieltoewijzing

Registreer klassieke Autopilot-apparaten via OEM of partner, of importeer de vereiste hardwaregegevens. Groepeer apparaten en wijs het juiste deployment profile toe. Controleer herkenning en profielstatus vóór verzending. Het verschijnen in een apparatenlijst betekent niet automatisch dat het gewenste profiel klaarstaat tijdens OOBE.

03. Pre-provisioning en ESP

Pre-provisioning verdeelt de installatie over een technicusfase en een gebruikersfase. De Enrollment Status Page kan wachten op geselecteerde essentiële apps en instellingen. Beperk blokkerende apps tot wat echt nodig is. Afhankelijkheden, detectieregels en netwerkverbindingen bepalen of de gebruiker door de setup komt.

04. Deployment onderzoeken

Controleer profiel, joinmodel, licentie, TPM-vereisten van de gekozen modus en bereikbaarheid van diensten. Onderzoek daarna de fase waarin ESP stopt. Gebruik deploymentrapportage en clientdiagnostiek. Test een representatief apparaat opnieuw na een wijziging, met dezelfde netwerkroute en appset als de echte gebruikers.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Registratie en profieltoewijzing moeten vóór OOBE kloppen.
  • Beperk ESP tot essentiële afhankelijkheden.
  • Autopilot vervangt geen OS-installatie op een lege schijf.

Samenvatting

Registratie en profieltoewijzing moeten vóór OOBE kloppen. Beperk ESP tot essentiële afhankelijkheden. Autopilot vervangt geen OS-installatie op een lege schijf. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Cursusverdieping per onderwerp (Engels) · 6 bronslides

Slide 49

Understand Windows Autopilot scenarios and benefits

  • Common deployment scenarios
  • User-driven : users sign in during OOBE and the device configures automatically
  • Self-deploying : provisions with little or no user interaction
  • Device preparation : a newer Windows 11 experience focused on faster setup
  • Existing devices : reimage current PCs and prepare them for Autopilot
  • Pre-provisioned : IT or partners complete setup before delivery
  • Autopilot Reset : reset a managed device for reuse without reimaging
  • Key benefits
  • Reduces custom image management
  • Speeds up device provisioning
  • Improves the user setup experience
  • Applies apps and policies consistently
  • Supports modern cloud-based device management
Slide 50

Register and import devices into Autopilot

  • Registration options
  • OEM or partner registration : devices added during purchase
  • CSV import : IT collects the hardware hash and uploads it in Intune
  • Direct import : IT imports the hash online with PowerShell
  • Group tags : group devices for profile assignment
  • Intune setup
  • Enable automatic MDM enrollment
  • Import devices in Windows enrollment
  • Assign an Autopilot deployment profile
  • Use groups to apply settings consistently
Slide 51

Configure Autopilot profiles and deployment modes

  • Autopilot deployment profiles define how registered devices are configured during OOBE.
  • Profile configuration
  • Create in Intune > Windows enrollment > Deployment profiles
  • Select the deployment mode and OOBE behavior
  • Configure language, keyboard, privacy, EULA, device name, and account options
  • Assign the profile to device groups
  • Optionally convert targeted corporate devices to Autopilot
  • Deployment modes
  • User-driven : user signs in and is associated with the device
  • Self-deploying : device provisions without user credentials
  • Microsoft Entra joined : recommended cloud-native join option
Slide 52

Pre-provision devices using Autopilot

  • Deployment is split into two phases
  • 1 Technician phase
  • Apps and policies applied by IT or a partner
  • Reseal
  • Prepared and boxed for delivery
  • 2 User phase
  • User signs in and final settings apply
  • Requirements
  • Enable pre-provisioning in the Autopilot profile
  • Supported Windows 10 or Windows 11 device
  • Microsoft Intune or supported MDM
  • TPM 2.0 with device attestation
  • Internet connectivity
  • Virtual machines are not supported
  • At OOBE
  • Press the Windows key five times → Windows Autopilot provisioning → Provision → Reseal
Slide 53

Assign profiles and monitor Autopilot deployments

  • Assign profiles
  • Assign Autopilot profiles to Microsoft Entra device groups
  • Intune applies the profile when registered devices join the assigned group
  • Confirm assignment in the Autopilot devices list
  • Check that Profile status shows Assigned
  • Monitor deployments
  • Use Windows Autopilot devices to review registration and profile details
  • Devices > Monitor > Windows Autopilot deployment status
  • Track provisioning progress, failures, and enrollment state
  • Deployment data is retained for 30 days
Slide 54

Troubleshoot Autopilot deployment issues

  • What to check
  • Profile & OOBE
  • Confirm the device received an Autopilot profile
  • Verify the expected OOBE screens and branding
  • Join & enrollment
  • Check Microsoft Entra join status
  • Confirm Intune enrollment completed
  • Network & requirements
  • Verify connectivity to required Autopilot services
  • Check licensing, permissions, and device limits
  • Tools & common errors
  • Diagnostic tools
  • Event Viewer : Provisioning-Diagnostics-Provider > Autopilot
  • Registry : HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Provisioning\Diagnostics\Autopilot
  • PowerShell : Get-AutopilotDiagnostics -Online
  • Common error codes
  • 801C0003 : Microsoft Entra join failed
  • 80180018 : MDM enrollment failed

1. Een medewerker ontvangt rechtstreeks een laptop en meldt zich tijdens OOBE aan. Welke klassieke modus past?

2. Een nieuwe laptop toont geen verwacht Autopilot-profiel. Wat controleer je eerst?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 55 · Question 1

Contoso is deploying 200 new Windows 11 laptops to remote employees. IT staff shouldn't need to touch or image each device before delivery. Which Windows Autopilot deployment mode should Contoso use?

  1. User-driven mode, so each employee completes setup by signing in with their organizational account during OOBE
  2. Self-deploying mode, so devices configure themselves without any user interaction
  3. Pre-provisioning mode, so IT staff completes app and policy installation before shipping to users
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: A. User-driven mode, so each employee completes setup by signing in with their organizational account during OOBE Correct. User-driven mode enables zero-touch provisioning. Devices are shipped directly to users who complete setup by signing in with their work account. The Autopilot service applies the deployment profile automatically during OOBE. B. Incorrect. Self-deploying mode is designed for shared or kiosk devices with no primary user. It requires TPM 2.0 device attestation and isn't appropriate for user-assigned laptops. C. Incorrect. Pre-provisioning reduces user-facing setup time but still requires IT staff to handle each device during the technician phase. The scenario specifies IT shouldn't touch each device.

Slide 55 · Question 2

A newly purchased device fails to receive its Autopilot deployment profile during OOBE. The device is connected to the internet and the user signed in with a valid organizational account. What is the most likely cause?

  1. The device hasn't been registered with the Windows Autopilot service
  2. The Autopilot deployment profile hasn't been assigned to a user group
  3. The Enrollment Status Page (ESP) is blocking profile delivery until apps are installed
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: A. The device hasn't been registered with the Windows Autopilot service Correct. A device must be registered with the Autopilot service before OOBE begins so the service can recognize it and deliver the correct deployment profile. B. Incorrect. Autopilot deployment profiles are assigned to device groups, not user groups. If the device were registered, a default profile or group assignment would still allow profile delivery. C. Incorrect. The ESP tracks app and policy installation progress after profile delivery. It doesn't prevent the profile itself from being received.

Slide 55 · Question 3

After completing the technician phase of a pre-provisioning deployment, the IT technician sees a red screen indicating failure. What should the technician check first?

  1. Whether the Autopilot deployment profile has the pre-provisioning option enabled
  2. Whether the device's TPM chip is version 1.2 or later
  3. Whether the user has an Intune license assigned in Microsoft Entra ID
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: A. Whether the Autopilot deployment profile has the pre-provisioning option enabled Correct. Pre-provisioning requires the deployment profile to have the 'Allow pre-provisioning' setting enabled. Without this setting, the technician phase can't complete. This is the most common configuration oversight. B. Incorrect. Pre-provisioning requires TPM 2.0 for device attestation, not version 1.2. However, checking the profile configuration is the first troubleshooting step before investigating hardware. C. Incorrect. The technician phase runs before any user is associated with the device. User licensing doesn't affect the technician phase.

Slide 56 · Question 1

Contoso needs to deploy dedicated kiosk devices at reception desks. Each kiosk should configure itself automatically with a specific app and kiosk profile, with no user sign-in required. Which Autopilot deployment mode is most appropriate?

  1. Self-deploying mode
  2. User-driven mode with a shared device configuration policy
  3. Pre-provisioning mode with IT completing setup before placement
Answer and source explanation

Q4 - Correct: A. Self-deploying mode Correct. Self-deploying mode provisions devices without any user interaction or credentials. It's designed for shared devices, kiosks, and digital signage where no user account is associated with the device. B. Incorrect. User-driven mode requires a user to sign in during OOBE. A shared device policy alone doesn't eliminate the user sign-in requirement during provisioning. C. Incorrect. Pre-provisioning still requires user interaction during the user phase. For fully automated, userless device configuration, self-deploying mode is the correct choice.

Slide 56 · Question 2

You assign a Windows Autopilot deployment profile to a dynamic Microsoft Entra device group. A newly registered Autopilot device doesn't receive the profile during OOBE, but it appears in the Autopilot devices list in Intune. What is the most likely reason?

  1. The device hasn't been added to the dynamic group because group membership evaluation hasn't completed
  2. The deployment profile requires manual approval before it can be assigned to new devices
  3. The Enrollment Status Page is preventing profile delivery until compliance policies are met
Answer and source explanation

Q5 - Correct: A. The device hasn't been added to the dynamic group because group membership evaluation hasn't completed Correct. Dynamic group membership evaluation can take several minutes to hours after registration. If OOBE begins before the device is added to the group, the profile assignment isn't applied. Wait for group membership to sync before powering on the device. B. Incorrect. Windows Autopilot deployment profiles don't have a manual approval step. Once a profile is assigned to a group, it applies automatically to all devices in that group. C. Incorrect. The ESP tracks installation progress during OOBE but doesn't block profile delivery. The issue is that the profile was never assigned because the device wasn't yet a group member when OOBE ran.
