Learning Path 05Module 02

AI en Copilot verantwoord toepassen

Je gebruikt AI als ondersteuning bij samenvatten en onderzoeken van beheer- en beveiligingssignalen.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Een overtuigend antwoord kan onvolledig of onjuist zijn. Beheerbeslissingen vragen controleerbare gegevens en menselijke verantwoordelijkheid.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: mogelijkheden afbakenen.
  • Uitleggen: gerichte vragen stellen.
  • Uitleggen: bevoegdheden en gegevens.
  • Uitleggen: van advies naar wijziging.

01. Mogelijkheden afbakenen

Security Copilot en functies in Intune kunnen context samenvatten en onderzoek ondersteunen. Beschikbaarheid hangt af van tenant, regio, licentie en uitrolstatus. Controleer de actuele mogelijkheden in de eigen omgeving. Bouw geen productieproces op een functie die alleen in een demonstratie of preview beschikbaar is.

02. Gerichte vragen stellen

Geef probleem, tijdvenster en gewenste vergelijking op. Vraag bijvoorbeeld welke instelling een apparaat noncompliant maakt en op welke gegevens dat antwoord steunt. Controleer de aangehaalde objecten en timestamps. Een brede vraag zonder scope kan gegevens uit verschillende oorzaken onterecht samenvoegen.

03. Bevoegdheden en gegevens

AI-ondersteuning werkt binnen geldende toegang en productvoorwaarden. Controleer rollen, scope en toegankelijke bronnen. Gebruik goedgekeurde kanalen voor gevoelige informatie en beperk gedeelde prompts tot noodzakelijke gegevens. Een extra interface mag geen reden zijn om onbeperkte beheerrechten te verlenen.

04. Van advies naar wijziging

Vergelijk een aanbeveling met Intune- en Defender-rapportage. Onderzoek de impact op echte doelgroepen en laat wijzigingen het normale changeproces volgen. Gebruik agents alleen met passende bevoegdheden en controle op hun acties. Verifieer na uitvoering het technische en zakelijke resultaat.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • AI-uitvoer is een onderzoekshulp en moet worden gecontroleerd.
  • Controleer beschikbaarheid en bevoegdheden.
  • Behoud pilot, impactanalyse en wijzigingsregistratie.

Samenvatting

AI-uitvoer is een onderzoekshulp en moet worden gecontroleerd. Controleer beschikbaarheid en bevoegdheden. Behoud pilot, impactanalyse en wijzigingsregistratie. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

1. Copilot stelt een beleidswijziging voor. Wat is de volgende stap?

2. Wat maakt een onderzoeksprompt bruikbaarder?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 22 · Question 1

An analyst opens a new incident in the Microsoft Defender portal that involves several devices and an obfuscated PowerShell script. What is the most appropriate first use of Microsoft Security Copilot?

  1. Automatically isolate every affected device based on Copilot's guided response recommendations.
  2. Generate an incident summary that organizes the attack overview, timeline, affected assets, and recommended actions, then ask follow-up questions to scope the investigation.
  3. Skip Copilot entirely and rely only on manual review of each alert because AI output cannot be trusted in security investigations.
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: B. Generate an incident summary that organizes the attack overview, timeline, affected assets, and recommended actions, then ask follow-up questions to scope the investigation. Security Copilot's incident summary gives analysts a faster starting point by consolidating the key incident details, and natural-language follow-up questions help narrow the investigation without switching between every alert and entity page. A. Guided response recommendations are investigation guidance. High-impact actions such as isolating production devices should be validated in Defender and follow the organization's incident response process before execution. C. Copilot is designed to accelerate investigation, not replace analyst judgment. Analysts use Copilot output as investigation support and then validate important findings before taking high-impact response actions.

Slide 22 · Question 2

A help desk technician reports that one Windows device receives a VPN profile while another similar device doesn't. Which Copilot in Intune capability most directly helps narrow the cause?

  1. Use Copilot to compare the two devices and identify differences in group membership, assigned policies, filters, and device attributes.
  2. Ask Copilot to automatically reassign the VPN configuration profile to every Windows device in the tenant.
  3. Run a Security Copilot promptbook for ransomware investigation against the affected device.
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: A. Use Copilot to compare the two devices and identify differences in group membership, assigned policies, filters, and device attributes. Device comparison in Copilot for Intune highlights similarities and differences across hardware, compliance, assignments, and group membership, which helps move from "this device is broken" to a concrete list of differences to investigate. B. Copilot doesn't make broad assignment changes on its own, and reassigning a profile to every device would bypass targeting, filters, and pilot testing rather than diagnose the actual cause. C. Promptbooks are repeatable security investigation workflows. A VPN assignment issue is an Intune configuration troubleshooting task, not a security incident.

Slide 22 · Question 3

Why is AI-driven prioritization especially valuable when IT operations and security teams share responsibility for endpoints?

  1. It removes the need for security teams to review high-risk threats because operations teams can handle every issue.
  2. It guarantees that no device will ever require manual investigation again.
  3. It gives both teams a shared view of device state and risk so operations can focus on reliability and policy health while security focuses on high-risk threats and incident response.
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: C. It gives both teams a shared view of device state and risk so operations can focus on reliability and policy health while security focuses on high-risk threats and incident response. Shared context such as device state, compliance posture, and recommendation history helps security and operations teams coordinate without conflicting priorities. A. AI doesn't shift accountability for security incidents to operations. Security teams still own high-risk threats and incident response. B. AI reduces routine sorting and analysis but doesn't eliminate manual investigation. Administrators still validate findings and handle exceptions.

Slide 23 · Question 1

Microsoft Defender shows that several devices in the same Intune group are involved in repeated alerts for the same suspicious behavior. Based on the unit guidance, what is the best next step for the endpoint administrator?

  1. Ignore the alerts because Intune already reports the devices as compliant.
  2. Treat the repeated alerts as a signal that an Intune policy or security control might need to change, and use device entity insights and advanced hunting to confirm the scope before adjusting policy.
  3. Immediately reimage every device in the group without further investigation.
Answer and source explanation

Q4 - Correct: B. Treat the repeated alerts as a signal that an Intune policy or security control might need to change, and use device entity insights and advanced hunting to confirm the scope before adjusting policy. Defender insights guide endpoint decisions. Repeated alerts across the same device group often point to a missing or weak control that should be addressed through Intune policy after the scope is confirmed. A. Compliance state alone doesn't reflect active risk. A device can be compliant and still be involved in incidents that require investigation or policy changes. C. Reimaging is a high-impact action that should be reserved for confirmed compromise. Administrators first investigate using device entity insights and advanced hunting to understand the scope and root cause.

Slide 23 · Question 2

An analyst wants to find every device in the environment that ran a specific suspicious file in the past two weeks. Which Microsoft Defender capability is designed for this question?

  1. Microsoft Secure Score recommendations.
  2. The threat analytics dashboard.
  3. Advanced hunting, optionally launched from an entity using the Go hunt action.
Answer and source explanation

Q5 - Correct: C. Advanced hunting, optionally launched from an entity using the Go hunt action. Advanced hunting is a query-based tool that lets analysts explore up to 30 days of raw data to find devices that ran a file, contacted a domain, or showed related activity. The Go hunt action scopes the query to a selected entity. A. Secure Score measures overall security posture and surfaces recommended improvement actions. It doesn't query historical device activity for a specific file. B. Threat analytics highlights active threats, campaigns, and exposed assets, but it doesn't run targeted queries against raw device activity data.

Learning Path 05Module 03

Endpointprestaties optimaliseren

Je maakt prestatiebeheer meetbaar met analytics, gerichte verbeteringen en herhaalbaar herstel.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Een veilig apparaat kan gebruikers toch hinderen door slechte prestaties. Verbeteringen moeten zichtbaar zijn in de dagelijkse ervaring.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: een uitgangsmeting kiezen.
  • Uitleggen: endpoint analytics interpreteren.
  • Uitleggen: remediations zorgvuldig ontwerpen.
  • Uitleggen: het effect bevestigen.

01. Een uitgangsmeting kiezen

Leg vast welke ervaring je verbetert: opstartduur, crashfrequentie of een terugkerend probleem. Selecteer een representatieve doelgroep en meetperiode. Controleer of telemetrie voldoende actueel en volledig is. Zonder nulmeting kun je een toevallige schommeling gemakkelijk verwarren met echte verbetering.

02. Endpoint analytics interpreteren

Gebruik scores als richting voor onderzoek en bekijk de onderliggende gegevens. Segmenteer op model, OS en appversie en vergelijk met de rest van de vloot. Een gemiddelde verbergt uitschieters. Prioriteer op getroffen gebruikers en de impact op werkzaamheden, niet alleen op een aantrekkelijk dashboardcijfer.

03. Remediations zorgvuldig ontwerpen

Laat detectie één duidelijke toestand testen en herstel alleen die toestand aanpassen. Maak het script herhaalbaar en kies de juiste context. Test foutafhandeling en rapporteer mislukking expliciet. Controleer de product- en licentievereisten voordat je een remediationpakket breed inzet.

04. Het effect bevestigen

Rol een verbetering eerst beperkt uit en meet dezelfde indicator opnieuw. Vergelijk met een passende controlegroep waar mogelijk. Breid alleen uit bij een aantoonbaar positief effect en houd regressies in het oog. Werk het beheerproces bij zodat dezelfde oorzaak niet telkens handmatig wordt opgelost.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Begin met een meetbare uitgangssituatie.
  • Een score vraagt onderzoek naar de onderliggende oorzaak.
  • Test herstel en meet het effect opnieuw.

Samenvatting

Begin met een meetbare uitgangssituatie. Een score vraagt onderzoek naar de onderliggende oorzaak. Test herstel en meet het effect opnieuw. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Slide 28

Configure remediation scripts using Microsoft Intune

  • Remediations run detection and remediation scripts on devices to find and fix issues automatically : before user impact.
  • 1 Detect
  • Detection script evaluates the device and identifies issues
  • 2 Evaluate
  • If an issue is found, remediation is required
  • 3 Remediate
  • Remediation script runs automatically to fix the issue
  • 4 Validate
  • Verify the device state and measure improvement
  • 5 Monitor & repeat
  • Track results in Endpoint Analytics and repeat as needed
  • Key benefits
  • Proactive issue detection and repair
  • Reduced help desk effort
  • Improved device health and performance
  • Safe to run repeatedly (idempotent)
  • Common use cases
  • Repair Windows Update components
  • Clear temporary files or caches
  • Validate and restore configuration settings
  • Disable problematic startup applications
Slide 29

Remediation scripts : create, requirements, and governance

  • Create a remediation package
  • Devices > Remediations
  • Create > Script package
  • Enter a name and description
  • Upload the detection script
  • Upload the remediation script
  • (Optional) Upload a script output file for logging
  • Assign to a pilot group first
  • Script requirements
  • Use PowerShell for both scripts · each < 64 KB
  • Detection returns 0 when no fix is needed; nonzero triggers remediation
  • Remediation runs silently within the timeout; scripts must be idempotent
  • Best practices & governance
  • Pilot before wide deployment; keep detection logic concise
  • Reuse vetted scripts; document behavior and rollback
  • Store scripts in source control; restrict who can deploy
  • Review results in Endpoint Analytics : success, failure, and error counts
Slide 30

Monitor endpoint reliability and user experience with endpoint analytics

  • User experience score
  • Single health indicator for device experience
  • Combines startup, app reliability, and device health
  • Low score = high remediation priority : focus on highest user impact
  • Typical actions
  • Remove high-impact startup apps
  • Update drivers and firmware
  • Optimize power settings and improve Windows Update readiness
  • Metric
  • Purpose
  • App reliability
  • Crashes and application hangs
  • Startup performance
  • Boot and sign-in times
  • Device health
  • Hardware, firmware, and update readiness
  • Recommended actions
  • Guided optimization and fixes

Kenniscontrole

Kies per vraag één antwoord. Je kunt opnieuw proberen; de score bepaalt je modulevoortgang niet.

1. Wat heb je nodig om het effect van een optimalisatie te beoordelen?

2. Wat is een goede remediation?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 31 · Question 1

A help desk analyst needs to investigate a single device that has unusually slow startup times and repeated app crashes, beyond what aggregated Endpoint Analytics reports can show. Which capability is designed for live, KQL-based investigation of one device at a time?

  1. The Endpoint Analytics Startup performance dashboard.
  2. Device Query (Microsoft Intune Suite / Device Query add-on).
  3. The App reliability report.
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: B. Device Query (Microsoft Intune Suite / Device Query add-on). Correct. Device Query lets you run real-time KQL queries against a single Windows device from the Intune admin center. It requires the Intune Suite or the Device Query add-on license. A. The Startup performance dashboard aggregates data across the fleet : it isn't a live, per-device KQL investigation tool. C. App reliability ranks apps by crash and hang counts across devices. It isn't a live, per-device investigation tool.

Slide 31 · Question 2

You're creating a detection script for an Intune Remediations package. What return-value contract must the detection script follow?

  1. Return 0 when no remediation is required and a nonzero value when the remediation script should run.
  2. Always return 0 and rely on Write-Output to signal whether remediation is needed.
  3. Return True or False from the script.
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: A. Return `0` when no remediation is required and a nonzero value when the remediation script should run. Correct. Intune Remediations interprets the detection script's exit code: `0` means healthy (no action), nonzero triggers the remediation script. B. Intune evaluates the detection script's exit code, not its stdout. A script that always exits `0` never triggers remediation. C. PowerShell scripts surface their result to Intune through the exit code, not a boolean return value.

Slide 31 · Question 3

You're rolling out a new Remediations package across the fleet. What's the recommended deployment approach?

  1. Assign the package to all devices immediately so the issue is fixed everywhere as quickly as possible.
  2. Pilot the package against a small group with varied hardware and update states, validate detection and remediation behavior, then expand.
  3. Run the remediation only as a one-off manual action from the admin center; don't assign it as a recurring package.
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: B. Pilot the package against a small group with varied hardware and update states, validate detection and remediation behavior, then expand. Correct. The recommended pattern is to pilot the package on a representative group, validate that detection fires only on affected devices and remediation succeeds, then expand the assignment. A. Pushing a remediation tenant-wide on day one risks side effects on hardware or configuration variants you didn't anticipate. The recommended approach is to pilot first. C. Remediations are designed to run automatically on a schedule against assigned devices. Treating them as one-off manual actions misses the point of the feature.

Slide 32 · Question 1

You want to find which devices in your tenant are most likely to be generating help desk tickets due to poor performance and reliability. Which Endpoint Analytics signal helps you prioritize?

  1. The list of installed Win32 apps per device.
  2. The User experience score : focus on the devices with the lowest scores first.
  3. The Intune license assignment report.
Answer and source explanation

Q4 - Correct: B. The User experience score : focus on the devices with the lowest scores first. Correct. The User experience score summarizes startup, app reliability, and device health into one indicator. Devices with the lowest scores are the most likely to drive support costs. A. Installed app inventory tells you what's deployed, not which devices are degrading the user experience. C. License assignment doesn't reflect device performance or reliability.

Slide 32 · Question 2

After deploying Endpoint Analytics recommended actions to address slow startup, what step ensures the change actually improved the user experience and didn't cause regression?

  1. Remove the recommended actions after one week regardless of results, to avoid unintended side effects.
  2. Re-check Endpoint Analytics metrics after the change, compare against the previous baseline, and document the outcome : repeating for any regressions.
  3. Disable Endpoint Analytics telemetry once the action is deployed, since the recommendation is now complete.
Answer and source explanation

Q5 - Correct: B. Re-check Endpoint Analytics metrics after the change, compare against the previous baseline, and document the outcome : repeating for any regressions. Correct. The structured remediation loop is: pilot → apply → re-check Endpoint Analytics metrics → compare to baseline → document. This confirms the improvement and catches regressions before broader rollout. A. Removing the change blindly defeats the purpose of the recommendation and discards the improvement you set out to make. C. Disabling telemetry blinds you to whether the change worked and prevents detection of future regressions.

Learning Path 06

Operationele kwaliteit en ondersteuning

3 modules · Nederlands met Engelstalige cursusverdieping

Learning Path 06Module 01

Rapportage en inzicht organiseren

Je zet Intune-rapporten, filters en exports om in bruikbare informatie voor beheer en stakeholders.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Een rapport is pas nuttig wanneer duidelijk is wie op afwijkingen reageert en hoe actueel de gegevens zijn.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: van vraag naar rapport.
  • Uitleggen: filters en interpretatie.
  • Uitleggen: workbooks en dashboards.
  • Uitleggen: veilig exporteren en plannen.

01. Van vraag naar rapport

Gebruik compliancegegevens voor beleidsnaleving, appstatus voor distributie en updategegevens voor patchbeheer. Kies operationele details voor directe opvolging en trends voor management. Vermeld de peildatum en scope. Gebruik Endpoint analytics voor ervaringsvragen, niet als vervanging van elk ander rapport.

02. Filters en interpretatie

Filter op relevante eigenschappen zoals platform, groep en status waar het rapport dit ondersteunt. Controleer aantallen en het effect van ontbrekende of oude gegevens. Een percentage zonder noemer kan misleiden. Maak herhaalbare weergaven waar de functie dat ondersteunt en documenteer de selectieregels.

03. Workbooks en dashboards

Aanvullende dashboards en Azure Monitor-workbooks kunnen gegevens uit ondersteunde bronnen samenbrengen. Controleer ingestie, kosten en toegangsrechten. Scheid de beheerder die een dashboard configureert van de doelgroep die het raadpleegt. Een visualisatie maakt foutieve brongegevens niet betrouwbaarder.

04. Veilig exporteren en plannen

Automatiseer terugkerende exports via geschikte rapportage- of Graph-mogelijkheden. Beperk opslag, bewaartermijn en ontvangers. Deel geen openbare links naar inventarisgegevens. Controleer of geplande jobs slagen en of de uitvoer volledig is; een geslaagde taak met nul onverwachte records vraagt onderzoek.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Kies een bron die de concrete vraag beantwoordt.
  • Vermeld scope, noemer en peildatum.
  • Beveilig exports en controleer geplande taken.

Samenvatting

Kies een bron die de concrete vraag beantwoordt. Vermeld scope, noemer en peildatum. Beveilig exports en controleer geplande taken. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Slide 5

Understand built-in reporting options in Microsoft Intune

  • Microsoft Intune reporting
  • Built-in reports fall into four categories : each answers a different operational question
  • Organizational
  • Tenant-wide posture across the fleet
  • Big-picture compliance and configuration health
  • Operational
  • Real-time, single-device detail
  • Live status for one device right now
  • Historical
  • Trends over time
  • Track change and catch regressions
  • Specialist
  • Bring-your-own-data tier
  • Custom analysis via Log Analytics and workbooks
  • Match the report category to the question you're answering : posture, live status, trend, or custom analysis.
Slide 6

Built-in reports : the catalog

  • Built-in report
  • Category
  • What it answers
  • Compliance
  • Organizational
  • Is the fleet at baseline?
  • Configuration
  • Operational
  • Which setting is failing?
  • Windows updates
  • Historical
  • Did the patch land?
  • Defender antivirus
  • Operational
  • Is antivirus alive and current?
  • Endpoint analytics
  • Specialist
  • Where is the user experience hurting?
  • Reading tip
  • Start broad with Compliance and Configuration reports for fleet posture, then drill into Windows updates, Defender antivirus, and Endpoint analytics to isolate the specific failure.
Slide 7

Customize Intune reports and filters for actionable insights

  • Customizing a report is three moves : narrow the data, surface the right columns, then save the view so you never rebuild it.
  • 1 · Filter
  • Narrow the dataset to what matters
  • Examples: BYOD only · iOS 17 · devices in an error state
  • 2 · Columns
  • Show what matters : add the fields you need
  • Add Primary User UPN · OS version · last check-in
  • 3 · Save view
  • Skip the rebuild tomorrow
  • Your filters and columns become a one-click dashboard
Slide 8

From report to action : three signals worth watching

  • Compliance trends
  • Signal : 5 → 500 noncompliant macOS overnight
  • Root cause : Apple shipped a new macOS that breaks the baseline
  • Action : update the policy once and fix the whole fleet
  • Enrollment failures
  • Signal : Autopilot timing out in one department
  • Root cause : ESP timeout: a massive new app blocks onboarding
  • Action : move the app to post-enrollment and raise the ESP timeout
  • Update success
  • Signal : feature update rolling back on older laptops
  • Root cause : driver conflict on a specific hardware generation
  • Action : pause that ring, test the driver, resume safely
Slide 9

Use Microsoft Intune workbooks and dashboards

  • Two ways to visualize Intune data : pick one by audience.
  • Admin center dashboard · personal & quick
  • Path: Intune admin center › Home › Edit
  • Device assignment status · profile success / error / conflict
  • App installation status across the fleet
  • Device compliance donut : compliant / non-compliant / in-grace
  • Bound to your admin profile : not sharable
  • Azure Monitor Workbooks · advanced & sharable
  • Text + KQL queries + rich charts on one canvas
  • Template: Windows Update for Business : missing patches, safeguard holds, velocity
  • Template: Intune Enrollment Activity : successes vs failures, bottlenecks
  • Community workbooks : LAPS rotations, app install histories
  • Prereq: route Intune Diagnostic Settings to a Log Analytics workspace first
Slide 10

Share workbooks with stakeholders

  • Workbooks only : admin-center dashboards can't be shared
  • 1 · Save
  • Save the workbook to a Resource Group in Azure
  • 2 · Permissions
  • Grant Log Analytics Reader via Azure RBAC
  • 3 · Share link
  • Send the Azure Portal URL : view-only access
  • Alternative : Power BI app
  • Connect Power BI to the Log Analytics workspace and publish executive reports straight into Microsoft 365 : ideal for leadership dashboards that live where stakeholders already work.
Slide 11

Export, schedule, and share reporting data securely

  • Three ways to get reporting data out of Intune : from ad-hoc to fully automated.
  • Manual CSV
  • Ad-hoc · zero setup
  • Click Export on any list : a .csv lands in your downloads
  • Diagnostic Settings
  • JSON stream · audit & sign-in logs
  • Pipe to Storage · Event Hubs · or a Log Analytics workspace
  • Data Warehouse
  • OData feed · ~30 days of history
  • Built for Power BI : trend analysis and scheduled refresh
Slide 12

Schedule the export : and guard it

  • Automate · put it on a schedule
  • Microsoft Graph API : Logic App or Automation Runbook: daily HTTP GET, email a DL, drop to SharePoint
  • PowerShell : Graph SDK + Scheduled Task on a server, or hand off to Azure Automation
  • Guard · share without leaking
  • Least privilege : Reports Reader · Global Reader · Log Analytics Reader
  • Audit access : Intune Audit Logs + App Registration sign-in logs
  • Minimize payload : filter before export; HR doesn't need IP, MAC, or installed apps
  • Method
  • Best for
  • Manual CSV
  • One-off pull : zero setup
  • Diagnostic Settings
  • Streaming to Storage, Event Hubs, or a SIEM
  • Data Warehouse
  • Power BI trend analysis (~30 days of history)
  • Graph API / PowerShell
  • Scheduled, automated delivery

1. Welk rapport kies je om falende apparaatvereisten te onderzoeken?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 13 · Question 1

Your security director asks for a single report showing which enrolled Windows devices are not compliant with your security baseline policy. Which Intune built-in report category is the most appropriate starting point?

  1. The Compliance reports category, which provides per-device and per-policy compliance state for all enrolled devices.
  2. The Endpoint analytics category, which scores device boot performance and application reliability.
  3. The Windows Update for Business reports category, which shows update deployment progress and error codes.
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: A. The Compliance reports category, which provides per-device and per-policy compliance state for all enrolled devices. Correct. The Compliance reports section in the Intune admin center surfaces device-level and policy-level compliance states, making it the direct source for identifying non-compliant devices and the specific policies they're failing. B. Incorrect. Endpoint analytics focuses on device experience metrics such as startup time and application crash rates : not on policy compliance state against a security baseline. C. Incorrect. Windows Update for Business reports focus on update rings and deployment status, not on security baseline policy compliance. The Compliance reports category is the right choice.

Slide 13 · Question 2

An administrator wants to see only the Intune compliance report rows for Windows devices in the Finance department that have been non-compliant for more than seven days, and wants to revisit this view weekly without reconfiguring it each time. What is the correct approach?

  1. Apply OS platform, department group, compliance status, and last-check-in date filters to the compliance report, then save the configuration as a named saved view.
  2. Export the full compliance report to CSV each week and sort the spreadsheet manually to isolate Finance devices.
  3. Create a new custom compliance policy scoped only to Finance devices so that a separate non-compliant count appears in the overview.
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: A. Apply OS platform, department group, compliance status, and last-check-in date filters to the compliance report, then save the configuration as a named saved view. Correct. Intune's report filter panel lets you stack multiple criteria (platform, group membership, status, date range). Saving the result as a named view means the filters are reapplied automatically on return visits, eliminating manual re-entry each week. B. Incorrect. Manual weekly exports with offline sorting is error-prone and time-consuming. The saved view feature in the Intune admin center is designed specifically to preserve filter combinations for recurring review. C. Incorrect. Creating a separate compliance policy changes your enforcement configuration, not just your reporting view. Saved views filter existing report data without modifying device policy assignments.

Slide 13 · Question 3

A compliance officer needs a monthly report on device compliance trends delivered to their email as a PDF, without requiring them to log into the Intune admin center. Which approach best meets this requirement?

  1. Use the Microsoft Graph API with a scheduled Azure Automation runbook to export the compliance report data monthly, format it, and send it via an email connector such as Logic Apps or Power Automate.
  2. Add the compliance officer as an Intune administrator so they can open the admin center and view reports directly each month.
  3. Configure Intune diagnostic settings to stream compliance data to an event hub, which will automatically email monthly PDFs.
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: A. Use the Microsoft Graph API with a scheduled Azure Automation runbook to export the compliance report data monthly, format it, and send it via an email connector such as Logic Apps or Power Automate. Correct. The Microsoft Graph API exposes Intune report data programmatically. An Azure Automation runbook scheduled monthly can pull the data, and a Logic Apps or Power Automate flow can format and deliver it to the compliance officer's email : without requiring portal access. B. Incorrect. Granting admin access violates least-privilege and still requires the officer to log into the portal. The requirement is for automated delivery to their email without portal access. C. Incorrect. Diagnostic settings to an event hub stream raw log data in near real-time for SIEM ingestion : they don't generate formatted monthly PDFs or send email. An automated export via Graph API and a workflow tool is the correct approach.

Learning Path 06Module 02

RBAC en gedelegeerd beheer

Je combineert rollen, scope groups en scope tags voor begrensde administratieve toegang.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Zonder geteste delegatie kan een regionaal helpdeskteam meer apparaten zien of wijzigen dan de organisatie bedoelt.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: rechten en scope scheiden.
  • Uitleggen: rollen toewijzen.
  • Uitleggen: regionale delegatie testen.
  • Uitleggen: audit en onderhoud.

01. Rechten en scope scheiden

Een Intune-rol bepaalt welke handelingen zijn toegestaan. Scope groups bepalen de gebruikers en apparaten waarop de opdracht betrekking heeft. Scope tags begrenzen de zichtbaarheid van ondersteunde objecten. Deze onderdelen vullen elkaar aan; geen enkel label vervangt zelfstandig een complete roltoewijzing.

02. Rollen toewijzen

Kies een passende ingebouwde rol of maak een beperkte custom rol. Wijs beheerdersgroepen toe in plaats van losse accounts waar dat past. Scheid lezen, aanpassen en destructieve acties. Onderzoek bestaande Entra- en Intune-rollen omdat opgetelde bevoegdheden de beoogde beperking kunnen doorbreken.

03. Regionale delegatie testen

Gebruik een afzonderlijk testaccount met alleen de bedoelde rol. Controleer zowel toegestane handelingen als geweigerde toegang buiten de regio. Let op zichtbaarheid van policies, apps en apparaten. Test opnieuw na veranderingen in groepen of tags en documenteer uitzonderingen op het delegatiemodel.

04. Audit en onderhoud

Lees auditlogs voor wijzigingen in rollen, policies en toewijzingen. Leg daarnaast de zakelijke reden en goedkeuring vast; auditlogs bevatten niet alle context. Herzie beheerdersrechten periodiek en trek tijdelijke toegang in. Bescherm escalatieroutes zodat een fout in scope niet tot blijvende onbeschikbaarheid leidt.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Rol, scope group en scope tag vervullen verschillende functies.
  • Test delegatie met een account zonder extra brede rollen.
  • Controleer zowel toegestane als geweigerde acties.

Samenvatting

Rol, scope group en scope tag vervullen verschillende functies. Test delegatie met een account zonder extra brede rollen. Controleer zowel toegestane als geweigerde acties. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Cursusverdieping per onderwerp (Engels) · 5 bronslides

Original course content. Text order follows the source presentation; visual tables and diagrams are represented as text.

Slide 16

Understand RBAC and scope tags

  • Two complementary controls : they answer different questions.
  • RBAC : what can they do?
  • Roles : a container of permissions (built-in or custom)
  • Custom roles : mix-and-match, e.g. Read configuration but Create compliance
  • Permissions : Create · Read · Update · Delete on each object type
  • Scope tags : which objects can they see?
  • Tag the object : a policy or app gets Redmond-IT or London-IT
  • Tag the role : the admin's assignment gets the same tag
  • The result : they see only matching objects; others are hidden
  • Where they meet : the role assignment binds all three
  • Members
  • who · IT staff / Entra group
  • +
  • Scope groups
  • manage which · users / devices
  • +
  • Scope tags
  • see which · matching objects
  • =
  • Least privilege
  • right person · right scope
Slide 17

Built-in roles : pick the narrowest fit

  • Built-in role
  • What it grants
  • Intune Role Administrator
  • The only role that grants RBAC : manage roles and assignments
  • Policy and Profile Manager
  • Compliance and configuration policies
  • Endpoint Security Manager
  • Security baselines and Defender
  • Help Desk Operator
  • Remote device tasks · no deletes
  • Read Only Operator
  • Auditors · view-only access
  • The Default scope tag : the gotcha
  • Every Intune object is tagged Default. To truly hide an object, remove the Default tag : otherwise any admin whose assignment includes Default can still see it.
Slide 18

Assign roles and permissions for multi-admin environments

  • Two ways to assign an Intune admin role : directly to a user, or to a role-assignable group.
  • Direct assignment
  • Grant a role explicitly to a single user account
  • Traditional method : intended for break-glass emergency accounts or very small teams
  • Permissions stay with the user permanently unless manually revoked
  • Hard to manage at scale : manual effort to track who your admins are
  • Group assignment · recommended
  • Assign roles to security groups : members inherit access automatically
  • Requires cloud-only groups with Entra roles can be assigned = Yes
  • Simplifies auditing : review membership, not individual accounts
  • Supports Identity Governance and Access Reviews
  • Requires Entra ID P1 or P2 for role-assignable groups
  • Best practice : use group assignment for scale and auditability; reserve direct assignment for break-glass accounts.
Slide 19

Configure scoped administration for regional or business unit separation

  • Scoped administration takes three steps : create tags, tag objects, then bind them to a role assignment.
  • Step 01 · Create tags
  • Go to Tenant administration › Roles
  • Define clear scope tags : e.g. Loc-Seattle or Dept-HR
  • Add descriptions so each tag's purpose is clear for auditing
  • Step 02 · Tag objects
  • Apply tags to profiles, policies, and apps via Properties
  • Critical: remove the Default tag for true object isolation
  • Tags can be set at creation or edited later
  • Step 03 · Bind roles
  • Configure the role assignment to link users, permissions, and scopes
  • Scope (Groups) : limit which users the admin can target
  • Scope (Tags) : define which objects the admin can see
  • Security
  • Remove the Default tag to hide objects from general admins : without it, scoping doesn't actually isolate anything.
Slide 20

Audit admin actions and monitor configuration changes

  • Audit and operational monitoring are critical to prevent configuration drift and detect unauthorized actions.
  • Audit logs · admin behavior
  • Records who changed what and when
  • Captures UPN, the specific action, the target object, and a timestamp
  • Operational logs · system behavior
  • Tracks enrollment failures and device sync issues
  • Helps correlate admin changes with system errors
  • Configuration drift detection
  • Alert with KQL queries in Log Analytics
  • Monitor scope tag modifications
  • Correlate Intune actions with PIM activations
  • Why it matters
  • Route Audit and Operational logs to Log Analytics so you can alert on drift, not just review it after the fact.

2. Hoe test je regionale delegatie betrouwbaar?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 21 · Question 1

Contoso has a Seattle help desk team that should be able to reset passcodes and reboot devices for users in the Seattle office, but should not be able to see or modify configuration profiles created for the New York office. What's the most appropriate way to delegate this access in Microsoft Intune?

  1. Assign the built-in Help Desk Operator role to a Seattle-IT security group, set Scope (Groups) to All Seattle Users, and set Scope (Tags) to a Seattle-Scope scope tag applied to Seattle-only objects.
  2. Assign the Global Administrator role directly to each Seattle help desk user so they can perform any device action when needed.
  3. Create a custom role that grants Read access to all configuration profiles, and assign it directly to each Seattle help desk user account.
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: A. Assign the built-in Help Desk Operator role to a Seattle-IT security group, set Scope (Groups) to All Seattle Users, and set Scope (Tags) to a Seattle-Scope scope tag applied to Seattle-only objects. Correct. The Help Desk Operator role provides only the device-action permissions needed (such as passcode reset and reboot). Scope (Groups) restricts which devices and users the admins can target, and Scope (Tags) restricts which Intune objects they can see : so New York configuration profiles remain hidden. B. Incorrect. Global Administrator grants tenant-wide permissions, which violates least-privilege and would let the Seattle team see and modify New York objects. Direct assignment also makes the access hard to manage at scale. C. Incorrect. Read access on all profiles would still expose New York configurations to the Seattle team. Direct assignment to individual user accounts also doesn't scale and complicates auditing of who has admin access.

Slide 21 · Question 2

An Intune administrator creates a new compliance policy and applies a custom scope tag named Executive-IT, but a regional admin without that tag can still see the policy. What's the most likely cause?

  1. The Default scope tag was not removed from the policy, so admins whose role assignments include the Default tag can still see the object.
  2. Custom scope tags only take effect after the regional admin signs out and back into the Intune admin center.
  3. Compliance policies don't honor scope tags; only configuration profiles and apps do.
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: A. The Default scope tag was not removed from the policy, so admins whose role assignments include the Default tag can still see the object. Correct. Every Intune object automatically receives the Default scope tag. To hide an object from admins who only have the Default tag, you must explicitly remove the Default tag and leave only the restricted custom tag (such as Executive-IT). B. Incorrect. Scope tag changes apply immediately to subsequent admin center loads : there's no separate sign-out requirement. The visibility is controlled by which tags exist on the object, not by session state. C. Incorrect. Scope tags apply to most Intune objects, including compliance policies, configuration profiles, and apps. The visibility issue here is caused by the Default tag still being present on the object.

Slide 22 · Question 1

Your security team wants to be alerted whenever an Intune admin deletes a device compliance policy, and they need to retain that audit history for several years to meet compliance requirements. Which combination of Intune diagnostic settings should you configure?

  1. Send AuditLogs to a Log Analytics workspace for KQL-based alerting on delete operations, and also archive AuditLogs to a storage account for long-term retention.
  2. Manually export the audit logs to CSV from the Intune admin center each week, and store the files on a shared file server.
  3. Stream AuditLogs to an event hub only, and rely on the third-party SIEM to handle both alerting and long-term retention.
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: A. Send AuditLogs to a Log Analytics workspace for KQL-based alerting on delete operations, and also archive AuditLogs to a storage account for long-term retention. Correct. Log Analytics enables KQL queries and Azure Monitor alert rules that can detect high-risk operations such as Delete DeviceCompliancePolicy. A storage account destination provides cheap, long-term cold storage to satisfy multi-year compliance retention. B. Incorrect. Manual CSV export doesn't provide automated alerting on delete operations and depends on someone remembering to export each week, which doesn't meet a continuous monitoring or guaranteed retention requirement. C. Incorrect. Streaming to an event hub is appropriate for SIEM integration, but it shifts both detection and retention responsibility entirely to the SIEM. For a Microsoft-native answer that explicitly meets both alerting and retention requirements, Log Analytics plus a storage account is the recommended pattern.

Learning Path 06Module 03

Tenantgezondheid en supportgereedheid

Je bewaakt dienstgezondheid, wijzigingen en supportinformatie als vast operationeel proces.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Veel individuele tickets kunnen door één service-incident of wijziging veroorzaakt worden. Goede voorbereiding voorkomt dubbel werk.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: gezondheid volgen.
  • Uitleggen: meldingen bruikbaar maken.
  • Uitleggen: supportdossiers voorbereiden.
  • Uitleggen: wijzigingen en baseline vastleggen.

01. Gezondheid volgen

Controleer Intune-tenantstatus, Microsoft 365 service health en relevante Message center-berichten. Maak onderscheid tussen een actief incident en een geplande verandering. Leg een controlefrequentie en eigenaar vast. Koppel brede gebruikersproblemen aan dienstmeldingen voordat je alle clients apart behandelt.

02. Meldingen bruikbaar maken

Kies signalen die tot een concrete actie leiden en stuur ze naar het juiste team. Maak afspraken over ernst, escalatie en bereikbaarheid. Controleer periodiek of notificaties werkelijk aankomen. Een te grote stroom ongerichte meldingen maakt het moeilijker urgente problemen te herkennen.

03. Supportdossiers voorbereiden

Verzamel scope, tijdstippen, foutcodes, apparaatidentificatoren en relevante logs. Noteer verwachte en werkelijke uitkomst en eerder uitgevoerde checks. Gebruik de ondersteunde supportkanalen in de tenant. Deel diagnostiek gecontroleerd en verwijder secrets uit bijlagen voordat je ze overdraagt.

04. Wijzigingen en baseline vastleggen

Beschrijf de uitgangssituatie, reden, goedkeuring, impact en herstelroute van een wijziging. Houd policies en kritieke afhankelijkheden bij in een actueel beheerdossier. Auditlogs vullen dit aan, maar vervangen geen changecontext. Evalueer incidenten om monitoring en procedures te verbeteren.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Controleer service health bij brede verstoringen.
  • Een melding heeft een eigenaar en opvolgactie nodig.
  • Auditlogs vervangen geen wijzigingsdossier.

Samenvatting

Controleer service health bij brede verstoringen. Een melding heeft een eigenaar en opvolgactie nodig. Auditlogs vervangen geen wijzigingsdossier. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Cursusverdieping per onderwerp (Engels) · 7 bronslides

Slide 25

Monitor tenant health and Intune service communications

  • Intune is a cloud SaaS service : its health depends on Microsoft's infrastructure. Know where to look and how to read the signals.
  • Tenant status · Intune
  • Tenant administration › Tenant status
  • Release version · connector health (Apple ADE, Managed Google Play) · service-health snapshot
  • Service health · Microsoft 365
  • Microsoft 365 admin center › Health › Service health
  • Current and historical health of all Microsoft 365 services
  • Signal
  • What it means
  • Response
  • Service incident
  • Critical, active impact
  • Stop local troubleshooting : monitor the incident
  • Service advisory
  • Minor or degraded feature
  • Stay aware · inform the helpdesk
  • Tenant action item
  • Needs admin action
  • Act : requires the Service support administrator role
  • Message center
  • Change-management announcements
  • Review deprecations, UI changes, new features
Slide 26

Read the signals on a regular cadence

  • Daily
  • 60-second Tenant status check
  • Connectors active · no active incidents
  • Weekly
  • Review the Message center
  • Scan upcoming changes and deprecations before they land
  • Automate
  • Configure email alerts
  • Route Service Health to a Teams channel via the Microsoft Graph service-communications API
  • Roles & access
  • Tenant action items and some Message center posts require the Service support administrator role : grant it deliberately to the operators who monitor tenant health.
Slide 27

Configure alerts and notifications in Microsoft Intune

  • Manual dashboard checks miss critical issues : configure proactive alerts across three layers.
  • 1 · Compliance failures
  • Actions for noncompliance with phased escalation
  • Day 0 push → Day 1 email user (CC helpdesk) → Day 3 final warning → Day 5 Conditional Access blocks + alerts Security
  • Prereq: a notification message template under Endpoint security › Device compliance › Notifications
  • 2 · Critical events & config drift
  • Intune has no native alert-rules engine
  • Route Audit + Operational logs to a Log Analytics workspace, then create alert rules
  • Examples: malware detected · Delete DeviceCompliancePolicy · >10 devices unenrolled in 1 hour
  • 3 · Service issues
  • Subscribe to Microsoft 365 service health
  • Health › Service health › Customize › Email
  • Notify your IT Operations DL of Intune incidents and advisories
Slide 28

Use support tools and troubleshoot service-related issues

  • Initial checklist
  • Check Service health : Tenant status + Microsoft 365
  • Verify licensing and Entra ID group membership
  • Troubleshooting + support › Troubleshoot for the affected user
  • Check the Device configuration report for policy conflicts
  • Windows client-side tools
  • dsregcmd /status : confirm AzureAdJoined / EnterpriseJoined = YES
  • Force MDM sync: Settings › Accounts › Access work or school › Sync
  • Event Viewer: DeviceManagement-Enterprise-Diagnostics-Provider › Admin
  • Collect diagnostics (remote action) or create a report locally
  • iOS / iPadOS / macOS / Android
  • Use the Company Portal app › Send Logs to upload diagnostics
  • Generates an Incident ID you can hand to support
Slide 29

Open a support request : Support Assistant

  • Start at Intune admin center › Troubleshooting + support › Help and support (or the ? icon) : three panes:
  • Find solutions
  • Self-help articles and guidance
  • An optional first stop before filing
  • Contact support
  • File a ticket and pick a severity
  • Premier / Unified unlock higher tiers and callbacks
  • Service requests
  • Track your open cases
  • Review status and history
  • Before you file
  • Always confirm service health first : many “issues” are active Microsoft incidents, and a ticket won't fix a platform outage.
Slide 30

Document tenant changes and establish operational baselines

  • A baseline is a snapshot of “normal” during stable operation : record metrics, alert on them, and compare weekly to catch regressions.
  • Device compliance
  • Target: 96% compliant
  • Enrollment success
  • 98% finish in < 45 min
  • App deployment
  • 99% of targeted devices
  • Endpoint analytics
  • Boot ~32 s · reliability 95/100
  • How to use a baseline
  • Record each metric, alert on it, and compare weekly : a regression is an unintended negative change from the baseline you can act on early.
Slide 31

Document every change : audit logs lack context

  • Why document changes
  • Audit logs show what / who / when : but not why, who approved, the expected impact, or how to roll back
  • Keep a formal change log in SharePoint, Jira, ServiceNow, or Excel
  • Context turns a raw audit trail into an actionable operational record
  • Change-log field
  • Captures
  • Date
  • When the change happened
  • Change / ticket ID
  • Traceability to the request
  • Admin
  • Who made the change
  • Component changed
  • What was modified
  • Description
  • What changed, in plain language
  • Business justification
  • Why the change was made
  • Expected impact / scope
  • Who and what is affected
  • Rollback plan
  • How to revert if needed

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 32 · Question 1

Users across multiple sites are suddenly unable to access corporate email on their Intune-managed devices. Before investigating device configuration or policy assignments, what should you check first?

  1. Review the Microsoft 365 Service Health dashboard in the admin center to determine whether an active Intune service incident is affecting device synchronization.
  2. Wipe and re-enroll all affected devices to force a fresh policy sync and restore email access.
  3. Open a Microsoft Support ticket immediately, because widespread failures are always caused by Microsoft infrastructure problems.
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: A. Review the Microsoft 365 Service Health dashboard in the admin center to determine whether an active Intune service incident is affecting device synchronization. Correct. Because Intune is a cloud SaaS solution, an active Microsoft service incident can cause widespread device sync failures that look like local configuration problems. Checking service health first avoids wasting time troubleshooting a misconfiguration when the root cause is a platform outage. B. Incorrect. Wiping devices is a destructive action that won't resolve a service-side outage and will cause more data loss. Service health should always be checked first before taking any remediation action on devices. C. Incorrect. Not every widespread failure is a Microsoft infrastructure issue: the cause could be a local network problem, a policy misconfiguration, or a certificate expiry. You should check the Service Health dashboard to confirm whether there's an active incident before opening a support ticket.

Slide 32 · Question 2

Your organization wants to be automatically notified by email whenever more than 10% of managed devices become non-compliant with the corporate security baseline. Which Intune feature should you configure to meet this requirement?

  1. Create an Intune notification message template, then configure a compliance policy with an action for noncompliance that triggers the email notification when the threshold is reached.
  2. Schedule a manual review of the Intune compliance report each day and send a team email if the non-compliant count exceeds the threshold.
  3. Configure a diagnostic settings export to a storage account and write a custom script to email the team when the CSV file changes.
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: A. Create an Intune notification message template, then configure a compliance policy with an action for noncompliance that triggers the email notification when the threshold is reached. Correct. Intune's compliance policy actions for noncompliance support sending email notifications, and notification message templates let you customize the recipient list and message body. This provides automated, policy-driven email alerts scoped to compliance state changes. B. Incorrect. Manual daily reviews don't provide automated alerting and are likely to miss threshold crossings that happen between review cycles. Compliance policy notification actions automate this without human intervention. C. Incorrect. Diagnostic settings to a storage account are designed for audit log archiving and long-term retention, not real-time compliance threshold alerting. Intune compliance policy notification actions are the correct built-in mechanism.

Slide 33 · Question 1

After a change management window, help desk calls spike with reports of failed device enrollments. You need to quickly identify whether a recent policy or configuration change caused the issue. What is the most efficient first step?

  1. Review the Intune audit logs filtered to the previous change window to see which administrative actions were performed, then cross-reference those changes with your documented operational baseline.
  2. Re-apply the enrollment restriction policies to all device platforms to reset them to their last known good state.
  3. Ask the change management team to verbally recall what was modified during the window, then test each recalled change one at a time.
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: A. Review the Intune audit logs filtered to the previous change window to see which administrative actions were performed, then cross-reference those changes with your documented operational baseline. Correct. Intune audit logs record every administrative action with a timestamp, actor, and affected resource. Filtering to the change window and comparing against a documented baseline lets you quickly pinpoint which recent change likely caused the enrollment failures, without guessing. B. Incorrect. Re-applying policies without first identifying the root cause could overwrite a correct configuration or introduce a second change, making the incident harder to diagnose. Reviewing audit logs is a non-destructive first step. C. Incorrect. Relying on verbal recall is error-prone and slow. Intune audit logs provide an authoritative, timestamped record of all admin actions that is far faster and more reliable than reconstructing changes from memory.

Learning Path 07

Mogelijkheden van Intune Suite

4 modules · Nederlands met Engelstalige cursusverdieping

Learning Path 07Module 01

Intune Suite verkennen

Je beoordeelt aanvullende beheer-, analyse- en ondersteuningsmogelijkheden op hun concrete meerwaarde.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Een suite is alleen nuttig wanneer functies aansluiten bij een aantoonbaar probleem en technisch en financieel haalbaar zijn.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: core en aanvullende mogelijkheden.
  • Uitleggen: functies aan scenario’s koppelen.
  • Uitleggen: technische en privacyvoorwaarden.
  • Uitleggen: gefaseerd invoeren.

01. Core en aanvullende mogelijkheden

Intune core levert basisbeheer, policies en rapportage. Intune Suite en losse add-ons breiden dat uit, bijvoorbeeld met Remote Help, Endpoint Privilege Management en Advanced Analytics. Controleer de actuele bundelinhoud en licentievoorwaarden. Niet iedere geavanceerde functie zit automatisch in iedere bestaande Microsoft 365-licentie.

02. Functies aan scenario’s koppelen

Remote Help ondersteunt gecontroleerde hulp op afstand. EPM beperkt permanente lokale adminrechten door gerichte elevatie. Advanced Analytics helpt complexere ervaringsproblemen onderzoeken. Enterprise App Management en Cloud PKI bedienen weer andere beheerbehoeften. Maak per functie expliciet welk proces verbetert en hoe je dat meet.

03. Technische en privacyvoorwaarden

Onderzoek ondersteunde platformen, clients, rollen, netwerkvereisten en benodigde gegevens. Een functienaam zegt niet dat elk platform dezelfde mogelijkheden heeft. Betrek privacy en supportprocessen bij de keuze. Zorg dat een uitbreiding geen onbedoeld brede toegang tot apparaten of gebruikersdata creëert.

04. Gefaseerd invoeren

Begin met een beperkt scenario en passende pilotgroep. Controleer licenties, train beheerders en verzamel gebruikersfeedback. Meet bijvoorbeeld oplostijd of vermindering van permanente adminrechten. Schaal alleen uit wanneer effect en beheerlast duidelijk zijn. Plan ook hoe je de toevoeging later onderhoudt of beëindigt.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Koppel iedere add-on aan een aantoonbare behoefte.
  • Controleer licentie en platformondersteuning.
  • Meet de waarde met een beperkte pilot.

Samenvatting

Koppel iedere add-on aan een aantoonbare behoefte. Controleer licentie en platformondersteuning. Meet de waarde met een beperkte pilot. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Slide 5

Understand the purpose and value of the Microsoft Intune Suite

  • Microsoft Intune Suite
  • Premium add-ons that extend core Intune : advanced management, security, and support
  • Advanced management
  • Advanced Analytics : AI-driven insights to anticipate and prevent device issues
  • Enterprise App Management : curated catalog of pre-configured Win32 apps
  • Security & Zero Trust
  • Endpoint Privilege Management : standard users elevate approved apps on demand
  • Cloud PKI : automates certificate issuance, renewal, and revocation
  • Support & connectivity
  • Remote Help : enterprise remote assistance integrated with Entra ID
  • Microsoft Tunnel for MAM : app-level VPN for unenrolled Android and iOS
  • Why it matters : stronger Zero Trust
  • Each add-on is licensed per user, individually or as the full Suite. Together the capabilities strengthen your Zero Trust architecture by extending your ability to follow Zero Trust principles across management, security, and support.

Slide 7

Compare Intune Suite features to core Intune capabilities

  • Remote assistance
  • Intune core
  • Basic remote actions: lock, wipe, sync, restart
  • View device info and initiate actions from the Intune admin center
  • Intune Suite
  • Screen sharing and full remote control
  • RBAC controls what helpers can do; compliance visibility before connecting
  • Elevation support; full session auditing; Entra ID authentication
  • Privilege management
  • Intune core
  • Deploy apps, scripts, and configs that run with system privileges during deployment
  • Intune Suite
  • Standard users elevate specific apps through defined rules
  • Automatic, user-confirmed, and support-approved workflows; granular controls by file attribute, path, and certificate
  • Reports all elevation activity; isolates processes with virtual accounts
Slide 8

Compare Intune Suite to core Intune : analytics and app deployment

  • Analytics
  • Intune core
  • Standard reporting on inventory, compliance, configuration, and app installations
  • Intune Suite
  • Anomaly detection for unusual performance patterns
  • Predictive analytics forecast which devices will have problems
  • Actionable recommendations and AI-driven insights from device health signals
  • Application deployment
  • Intune core
  • Win32 deployment with full control over packages, detection rules, requirements, and uninstall
  • Intune Suite
  • Pre-configured apps in the Enterprise App Catalog
  • Default install settings and detection rules; Microsoft-hosted content
  • Simplified deployment for popular business apps
Slide 9

Plan licensing and deployment strategies for Intune Suite

  • License Intune Suite add-ons per user : individually or as the full bundle.
  • Individual add-ons
  • License only what you need, per user:
  • Endpoint Privilege Management · Enterprise App Management
  • Advanced Analytics · Remote Help · Cloud PKI
  • Microsoft Tunnel for MAM · Firmware Over-the-Air
  • Specialty devices management
  • Full Intune Suite bundle
  • All add-ons in a single per-user license
  • If you need more than a few capabilities, the Suite is usually more cost-effective than stacking individual add-ons
  • One SKU to manage and renew
  • Rule of thumb: several add-ons → buy the Suite

Slide 11 · Question 1

Contoso wants to remove local administrator rights from end users but still allow them to install approved business apps on demand without filing a helpdesk ticket. Which Microsoft Intune Suite component most directly addresses this requirement?

  1. Remote Help
  2. Endpoint Privilege Management
  3. Advanced Analytics
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: B. Endpoint Privilege Management Correct. Endpoint Privilege Management enables Zero Trust by allowing users to run as standard users while still elevating specific approved applications under admin-defined policy. A. Remote Help provides secure remote assistance from support staff but doesn't grant on-demand elevation rights to end users for approved apps. C. Advanced Analytics provides reporting and proactive insights on device health, but doesn't control privilege elevation.

Slide 11 · Question 2

Which statement best describes the relationship between core Microsoft Intune and the Microsoft Intune Suite?

  1. The Microsoft Intune Suite replaces core Intune functionality with premium versions of the same features.
  2. The Microsoft Intune Suite is a separate product that doesn't integrate with core Intune.
  3. The Microsoft Intune Suite is a set of premium add-on capabilities that extends core Intune for advanced endpoint management scenarios.
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: C. The Microsoft Intune Suite is a set of premium add-on capabilities that extends core Intune for advanced endpoint management scenarios. Correct. The Suite layers on top of core Intune to add advanced capabilities such as Remote Help, Endpoint Privilege Management, and Advanced Analytics. A. The Suite doesn't replace core Intune. Core Intune capabilities such as device enrollment, compliance, and configuration profiles remain unchanged. B. Suite components are fully integrated with core Intune and are managed from the same Intune admin center.

Slide 11 · Question 3

An organization wants to evaluate a single Microsoft Intune Suite add-on before committing to the full Suite license. Which licensing approach best supports this goal?

  1. Purchase the full Microsoft Intune Suite license and disable the features they don't want.
  2. License the individual add-on standalone, or start a free trial for that capability.
  3. Wait for the capability to be added to core Intune at no additional cost.
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: B. License the individual add-on standalone, or start a free trial for that capability. Correct. Intune Suite add-ons can be licensed individually, and free trials let organizations evaluate a capability before purchasing. A. Purchasing the full Suite to disable features is more expensive than necessary when only one add-on is needed. C. Suite add-ons are premium capabilities and aren't added to core Intune. Organizations that need them must license them separately or as part of the Suite.

Learning Path 07Module 02

Remote Help implementeren

Je organiseert identiteitsgebonden ondersteuning op afstand met passende rechten en controle.

← Vorige moduleVolgende module →↑ Terug naar boven

Waarom is deze module belangrijk?

Schermtoegang en elevatie raken privacy en beveiliging en vragen meer dan alleen een technische verbinding.

Leerdoelen

  • Uitleggen: sessiemodel en identiteit.
  • Uitleggen: configuratie en distributie.
  • Uitleggen: ondersteuning uitvoeren.
  • Uitleggen: toezicht en privacy.

01. Sessiemodel en identiteit

Remote Help gebruikt organisatie-identiteit voor helper en gebruiker in ondersteunde scenario’s. Controleer of de gewenste platformfuncties beschikbaar zijn en welke toestemming nodig is. Maak gebruikers duidelijk wie helpt en wat de sessie toestaat. Houd rekening met verschillen tussen begeleide en specifiek ondersteunde onbegeleide scenario’s.

02. Configuratie en distributie

Controleer tenantinstellingen, licenties en netwerkvereisten. Distribueer de vereiste client en verifieer versies. Gebruik passende Intune-rollen en scopes voor helpers. Maak onderscheid tussen bekijken, overnemen en elevatie; geef niet iedere medewerker automatisch alle drie de mogelijkheden.

03. Ondersteuning uitvoeren

Bevestig identiteit, ticket en probleem voordat de sessie begint. Laat de gebruiker toestemming geven waar de gekozen modus dat vereist. Voer alleen noodzakelijke acties uit en beëindig de verbinding na afloop. Een technisch bereikbare computer is nog geen toestemming om onbeperkt mee te kijken.

04. Toezicht en privacy

Controleer sessiegegevens en rolgebruik in beschikbare rapporten. Leg bewaartermijnen en interne gedragsregels vast. Sessiemetadata is niet hetzelfde als een volledige schermopname. Onderzoek mislukte verbindingen via identiteit, client, netwerk en rechten voordat je beveiligingsinstellingen verzwakt.

Wat moet je als beheerder onthouden?

  • Scheid bekijken, overnemen en elevatie.
  • Controleer platformmogelijkheden en licenties.
  • Koppel sessies aan een legitiem supportproces.

Samenvatting

Scheid bekijken, overnemen en elevatie. Controleer platformmogelijkheden en licenties. Koppel sessies aan een legitiem supportproces. Gebruik de hoofdstukken om keuzes te onderbouwen en controleer altijd de effectieve toestand naast de portalstatus.

Slide 14

Understand Remote Help and its role in end-user support

  • Remote Help
  • Secure, real-time remote assistance for Intune-managed support scenarios
  • What helpers can do
  • View the user's screen, request control, and perform support actions
  • Troubleshoot apps, settings, configuration, and user errors
  • Best when chat or written instructions aren't enough
  • Secure & transparent
  • Uses Microsoft Entra ID identities and Intune RBAC to control helper permissions
  • Transparent sessions : users can approve, decline, or end assistance
  • Reporting captures metadata: helper, sharer, device, time, control type
  • Before you start
  • Requires proper licensing
  • Role assignment and scope configuration
  • Privacy controls in place
Slide 15

How a Remote Help session works

  • Request
  • User requests help
  • Authenticate
  • One-time code + Entra sign-in
  • Consent
  • Approve view or control
  • Assist
  • Helper troubleshoots + elevates
  • Resolve
  • End when resolved
  • Transparent by design
  • The sharer stays in control throughout : they approve the level of access before the helper connects and can end the session at any time. Remote Help stores session metadata only, never recordings, screen images, or keystrokes.
Slide 16

Configure and deploy Remote Help in Microsoft Intune

  • Setting up Remote Help is a sequence : turn it on, license it, then control who can help and how much they can do.
  • 1 · Enable & license
  • Enable in Tenant administration › Remote Help › Settings
  • Requires Remote Help licensing for both helpers and sharers
  • Helpers and sharers sign in with organizational Entra ID accounts
  • 2 · Configure tenant
  • Set unenrolled device support
  • Set chat behavior for sessions
  • 3 · Assign permissions (RBAC)
  • Assign helper permissions via Intune RBAC or custom roles
  • Least privilege : separate view screen, full control, elevation, and unattended access
Slide 17

Deploy the Remote Help app and validate requirements

  • Deploy the app
  • Push the Remote Help app to Windows, macOS, and Android Enterprise dedicated devices
  • Use scope groups to control which users or devices a helper can support
  • Validate & test
  • Confirm platform requirements are met on each OS
  • Test deployment and permissions before rolling out broadly
  • Platform
  • Requirement to validate
  • Windows
  • WebView2 runtime installed and updated
  • macOS
  • Privacy permissions granted (screen recording, accessibility)
  • Android Enterprise
  • Dedicated devices; app permissions granted
Slide 18

Support and monitor Remote Help sessions

  • Active support workflow
  • Start from the Remote Help app or a supported enrolled device in Intune
  • Confirm the user, device, issue, and reason for support
  • Begin with screen sharing; request full control only when needed
  • Use elevation on Windows only when the helper has permission
  • End the session as soon as the issue is resolved
  • Troubleshooting checks
  • Check
  • What to verify
  • Device state
  • Enrolled, online, healthy, registered with Entra ID
  • App readiness
  • Remote Help app installed and updated
  • Helper access
  • Correct Intune RBAC permissions and scope
  • Policy path
  • Device policy doesn't block the support connection
Slide 19

Evaluate privacy, permissions, and compliance considerations

  • Transparency & consent
  • Keep sessions transparent and user-approved
  • Show helper and sharer identity before the session continues
  • Users can approve, decline, or end a session
  • Least-privilege access
  • Use least-privileged Intune RBAC for support staff
  • Limit helper scope to the users and device groups they support
  • Separate view screen, full control, elevation, and unattended permissions
  • Treat elevation as privileged : restrict it to trusted roles
  • Audit & compliance
  • Review activity with session reports and audit logs
  • Validate sessions align with device compliance, support tickets, and policy
  • Remote Help stores session metadata, not recordings, screen images, or keystrokes
  • Privacy by design
  • Consent, least privilege, and metadata-only logging keep support auditable without capturing what the user sees or types.

1. Een helper moet alleen het scherm bekijken. Welke rechten kies je?

2. Wat controleer je vóór een supportsessie?

Oorspronkelijke kennisvragen uit de cursus (Engels)

Unchanged question wording from the source decks, apart from typography. Answers and explanations, where present, come from the associated speaker notes. Treat them as course-source answers; validate current product behavior in Microsoft documentation.

Slide 20 · Question 1

A support agent at Contoso needs to view a user's screen and perform an elevated action to fix a misconfigured app. Which two Remote Help participant roles are involved in this session?

  1. Administrator and auditor
  2. Helper and sharer
  3. Owner and guest
Answer and source explanation

Q1 - Correct: B. Helper and sharer Correct. The helper is the support agent who provides assistance, and the sharer is the user receiving help and sharing their screen. A. Administrators configure Remote Help and assign permissions, but they aren't the two participants in the session itself. There's no separate auditor role. C. Owner and guest aren't Remote Help roles. Remote Help uses helper, sharer, and administrator roles.

Slide 20 · Question 2

Where do you assign helper and full-control permissions for Microsoft Intune Remote Help?

  1. By configuring local administrator group membership on each device.
  2. By using Intune role-based access control (RBAC) to assign Remote Help roles to Microsoft Entra ID groups.
  3. By granting Global Administrator rights to support staff in the Microsoft Entra admin center.
Answer and source explanation

Q2 - Correct: B. By using Intune role-based access control (RBAC) to assign Remote Help roles to Microsoft Entra ID groups. Correct. Remote Help permissions are assigned through Intune RBAC, scoped to Microsoft Entra ID groups of helpers. A. Remote Help permissions aren't managed through local group membership on the device. C. Granting Global Administrator is over-privileged and unnecessary. Remote Help uses scoped Intune RBAC roles.

Slide 20 · Question 3

A compliance officer asks how end users are informed before a helper takes control of their device during a Remote Help session. What's the correct answer?

  1. The helper takes control silently; users are notified by email afterward.
  2. The sharer sees a consent prompt and must approve the level of access : view-only or full control : before the helper proceeds.
  3. Administrators must call the user by phone to authorize each session.
Answer and source explanation

Q3 - Correct: B. The sharer sees a consent prompt and must approve the level of access : view-only or full control : before the helper proceeds. Correct. Remote Help requires explicit sharer consent before each session, and the sharer chooses whether to allow view-only or full control. A. Remote Help doesn't grant silent control. The sharer must explicitly consent before any view or control action. C. Phone authorization isn't required. Consent is built into the Remote Help session prompt presented to the sharer.
